— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* `learner-model` gets the graphs of the current versions of all the modules whose evidence is in the profile's log — whether they are connected or not and whether the current version suits the learner. The engine, as before, gets the graphs of the current versions of the connected modules that suit the learner. Otherwise the set of graphs would depend on the connections and on the profile's age category and languages, and the cache mark does not carry them: after a module is disconnected or the category changes, the appended state would silently diverge from the recomputed one (В4 in table 3 of RUN.md). Now the set is given by the log, the release and the window, and the cache mark stays as it was (R06). A module without a current version gives no graph, and its evidence waits (R08). A connected module about which there is no evidence yet does not enter the cache: `learner-model` gets its graph at reading, together with the engine. What depends on the connections — the starting point of skills without evidence and the mastery of an anchor skill by the skills "in the learner's modules" ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 13), — `learner-model` computes at reading and does not write to the cache (R08). Rejected — a hash of the set of the graphs' digests in the cache mark: each reconnection would recompute the whole log, and a disconnected module would have no state until it is connected again. The author's decision by a finding of the independent review in the planning of T04. — R08; RUN.md, T04; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.3 and 3.4.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* two findings of the independent review in the planning of T12, the author's decisions together with the plan:
- the check on request closes an attempt or a lesson run "unchecked" only when the image's own index has marked the version withdrawn or its format version is outside this image's window. A package digest that the image's index does not know — this is the previous image during a deployment and a package that the new release brought — gets a temporary refusal without a record, and a repeat of the request will reach the new image. Otherwise the previous image would close the attempts and lessons of the new one "unchecked". The index is cumulative (R63 spec), so the new image knows the digests of all the previous ones. A rollback to an image older than the release on which attempts were begun is the case of В7: `api` is rolled back only by rolling forward, and until then such attempts and runs get a temporary refusal and wait;
- whether an open lesson run suits a learner, `api` judges thus: the module by the current version, as before, and the lesson and each of its tasks by the run's package, the one the learner sees (R13). A new run and an attempt outside a lesson by the current version, as before.

— R13; RUN.md, T12; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.3 and 3.7.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* `app` learns of a withdrawal of a version by a request of its own — "which of these digests does the image no longer give", — and does not infer it from a refusal: it takes open views from the cache and does not reach `api` with them (Д68 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`). This replaces "`app`, on the refusal, deletes the version's open views from the cache" in this entry. The refusals stay, so that a withdrawal is visible in the middle of work too: for an open view, the cause "version withdrawn", for a file of the device part, the answer `410`. A digest that the image's index does not know is a temporary refusal without a record, as the amendment above decided: for a method, `unavailable`, for a file, `503` with `Retry-After`. The author's decision in the planning of T07. — R18; RUN.md, T07; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.3 and 3.9.

— *Amendment 2026-11-07:* from `spec` v0.9.0 the format version is 0.3 (R101 spec), and "while the format is v0, only 0.2" above means "only the current format version": on v0.9.4 this is 0.3. The newest version that is not withdrawn, for a new attempt, is chosen by a function of the `module-kit` library — the package `moduleindex`, — and not by the own code of `api` (the card architecture 3.7 spec), and module versions are compared by the format, [format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 27: in 0.y.z, y plays the major, and while the format is v0, a module's move to a new format version is a major change (R89 spec). The rule of the current version and the window this does not change. — R23; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.3.

— *Amendment 2026-11-08:* the image of `api` is built by the deployment of `infra`, not by the release of `api`: the release puts out a signed static binary and the `Dockerfile`, the deployment checks them by `release-verify`, builds the image on `gcr.io/distroless/static-debian13:nonroot` by digest and keeps it in a closed registry (R105, R106 spec; Т-19 closed). The module packages are put into the image by the code of `api`: the build stage of the `Dockerfile` calls a command of the binary that reads the embedded `modules.json` and does the steps of "Image build" — the same command by which the recipe of the first app puts the packages into a folder. The deployment does not repeat the logic of packages. "The image build reads the same file" above means this command. The rollback only by rolling forward was recorded by R106 spec, and whether to reject a release of `api` lower than the one rolled out is decided by T02 `infra`. — R24; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1 and 3.3.

— *Amendment 2026-11-09* (T30; R40): from the line of `spec` 0.10 the format window of `api` is 0.4 (the 2026-11-09 amendment to R44 spec), and the vectors of `spec.Format` are only 0.4. The releases of `modules` from `v0.1.0` to `v0.1.4` carry packages of 0.3, outside the window, so `modules.json` will pin only a release where the modules have moved to 0.4 (row 3.9 of `COORDINATION.md` of `spec`). There is nothing yet to read packages of 0.4 with: `module-kit` `v0.2.6` reads 0.3 and does not build beside `spec` v0.12.0 — `module`, `modulepackage`, `moduleindex` and `moduleversion` fail on two fields of the format that changed type ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.3). The command that puts the packages into the image, their reading and the version of a new attempt wait for a release of `module-kit` on the line of `spec` 0.10 or newer — the rows 3.8.

— *Amendment 2026-11-09* (T31; R41): `modules.json` pins `modules` v0.1.4 — the latest release as of 2026-11-09, signed by the shared `release.yml` of `.github` v0.1.15, — and in the window 0.4 it has not a single package: all four of its entries are of format 0.3. The image and the folder of the first app hold only the index, the modules have no current version, and packages of 0.4 will be pinned by a pull request to the release of epic E7 of `modules` (row 3.9 of `COORDINATION.md` of `spec`). The wait for rows 3.8 from the 2026-11-09 amendment above has ended: `module-kit` v0.3.0 came out on `spec` v0.12.0 and reads format 0.4 (R52 module-kit). How the folder, the command and the check in CI are built is R41.

— *Amendment 2026-11-09* (T32; R42): "Start and serving" in code. Open views, `CheckPackages` and the file path answer by the status of a digest in the image's index: "gives" — the package is in the window, and the entry is not withdrawn; "no longer gives" — the index knows the digest, and the entry is withdrawn or outside the window; "does not know" — the digest is not in the index. A digest that two entries name "gives" if at least one of them gives it. The current version of a module for a learner is `moduleindex.Newest` and the audience of its manifest: if it does not suit the learner, for them the module is without a current version, and `api` does not give the previous version. How this is built is R42.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10* (T34; R44): a new attempt outside a lesson in code. `BeginAttempt` takes the current version of a module for the learner by the same rule as the catalog — `packages.Set.CurrentFor`, — and the attempt stores the package digest and the versions of the module, the format and `module-kit`. The check on request closes an attempt on a package that the image no longer gives "unchecked", and for a package that the image's index does not know answers `CAUSE_PACKAGE_UNKNOWN` without a record. Runs on such a package are ended by T38, and the words of hints at closing are deleted by T37.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10* (T41; R51): the job after the deployment in code, by the command `api after`, which the deployment of `infra` calls with the role `after` when the new revision has taken all the traffic. It closes "unchecked" the open attempts and runs on packages that the image's index knows but no longer gives, one at a time, in the order of the request, and writes their evidence; a digest that the index does not know it does not touch. The same steps run in the daily `api sweep` too, before the closing of abandoned ones, — this is the safety net of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.3.

— *Amendment 2026-11-11* (T44; R53): `modules.json` pins `modules` v0.2.5, where there are five packages in the window 0.4, and each of the three modules has a current version. The image and the folder of the first app hold packages, not only the index, as the 2026-11-09 amendment (T31) said.

— *Amendment 2026-11-12* (T57; R61): the delay of a withdrawal until the author's pull request is no longer silent. The job "Withdrawals" once a day takes the newest release of `modules`, checks the signature of its index by `release-verify` and fails while the newest index withdraws a version that the pinned release gives. A withdrawal still takes effect only with a pull request that pins a release with the mark — the rule of this entry does not change. Д9 is closed.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14* (T76; R71): `modules.json` pins `modules` v0.2.7, where there are six packages in the window 0.4: the five of v0.2.5 and the module of `tutor`'s board, so four modules, each with a current version.

## Carrying progress over

**R08. Carrying progress over after a major version of a module is not a separate step but part of each computation of the state from the log: at a recomputation and at an append, `learner-model` routes each piece of evidence by `retired_skills` of the graph of the current version, and the log does not change. Evidence about a skill without a pair goes to no skill, and about a skill that the graph of the current version does not know, it waits in the log until the next version names it: so also after a withdrawal of the newest major.**
- **What was decided** ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.4; sources — section 4):
  - when: carrying over happens each time `learner-model` computes the state from the log. This is the first read of a profile's model after the deployment of an image that changed the cache mark (R06, R07), and each append of evidence — that of an attempt begun on the previous version of the module included (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R06; В4 in table 3 of RUN.md). There is no separate job or migration, and the log does not change (R06);
  - over what: over the profile's log and the graphs of the current versions of the log's modules (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R07). Only the table `retired_skills` of the current version is needed: the record of a retired skill stays in all following versions, and `replaced_by` names the skills of this version and, when a named skill is later retired itself, its replacements instead ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 12). So evidence of any age is carried over without the old graphs, and the packages that have left the image (R07) are not needed for carrying over — if the evidence carries everything the recomputation reads (В8 in table 3 of RUN.md);
  - who does what. `api` cuts off the `wrong_key` evidence by its column — the package digest (R06, R07), — gives `learner-model` the log and the graphs in its types, with `retired_skills` (R28 spec), writes the result into the cache with the mark, and gives the engine the graphs of the connected modules that suit the learner. The graph of a connected module about which there is no evidence yet `learner-model` gets at a read too, but it does not go into the cache. Carrying over is done by `learner-model` (architecture 3.8 spec): each module skill ID in a piece of evidence it looks up in the graph of the current version of the module that this ID names by its full ID (concepts 3 spec):
    - the skill is in `skills` — the evidence goes to it: the meaning of a skill under the same ID does not change ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 12);
    - the skill is in `retired_skills` — it goes to each skill from `replaced_by`, and with what weight is decided by `learner-model`. `[]` — to none;
    - in neither — it goes to no skill, without an error, and the row stays in the log;
    - an anchor skill `core.<name>` goes as it is, if the vocabulary of the format version that `api` reads knows it: it can be a supporting skill of a task (the schema `task.schema.json`, `supporting`), it has no module, and `retired_skills` does not concern it ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 13). An anchor skill that the vocabulary no longer knows goes nowhere: there is no table for anchor skills, and a new meaning comes under a new ID (R50 spec);
    - the skills of a trap, if the evidence carries them, by the same rule. The trap ID itself is not reused, and it needs no table ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 16): a trap that is not in the catalog of the current version is not among the repeating traps — there is nothing to show it with.

    What this needs from `learner-model` is В8, «в реестр spec»;
  - a skill without a pair, `replaced_by: []`: the progress in it goes nowhere ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 12). It is not on the skill map — the map draws the graph of the current version, and `retired_skills` is closed and carries no title of the skill. Its evidence stays in the log and goes into the export under the earlier full ID. There is no notice to the learner or the adult;
  - a withdrawal of the newest major, when a version of the previous major becomes current (R07; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.3), goes by the same rule. The evidence about skills that are in the previous major under the same ID goes to them as usual, and about skills that it does not know, it waits: it stays in the log, goes nowhere and is not visible on the map. When the next version names such a skill in `skills` or `retired_skills`, its release changes the cache mark, and the recomputation counts it. With `wrong_key` the filter cuts it off anyway. The waiting evidence comes back only if version comparison counts the withdrawn version as published — В9, «в реестр spec». The evidence of a module without a current version waits the same way: it gives no graph (R07);
  - repeatability (R03 spec): the same prefix of the log up to number N, the same `wrong_key` marks of the pinned index, the same graphs of the current versions and the same version of `learner-model` give the same carrying over, and the cache mark and the number of the last evidence counted hold them. The engine choice stores the release of `modules`, the number of the last evidence and the package digests (R06) — the digests of all the graphs that `learner-model` and the engine got, — so carrying over repeats in a repeat of the choice too. In the cache is only what the log, the release, the window and the version of `learner-model` define: the state of the skills of the log's modules. What depends on connections and the profile — the starting point of skills without evidence, including in a connected module about which there is no evidence yet, and the mastery of an anchor skill by the skills "in the learner's modules" ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 13), — `learner-model` computes at a read, by the graphs that `api` gives at that time, and does not write into the cache. Time is not carrying over either: forgetting is decided by T02 of `learner-model`. If any of this goes into the cache, the question comes back to В4. A snapshot of the state, if T04 of `learner-model` introduces one, holds only under the same mark, and carrying over does not apply to it: the log is carried over, not what was computed (R06);
  - on the map after carrying over — the graph of the current version and the state computed from the log. By the format, a renamed skill gets the evidence of the earlier one, a split one — each of its parts, a merged one — the evidence of all, and with what weight and whether "mastered" carries over is decided by `learner-model` (architecture 3.8 spec). A skill without a pair and a waiting skill are not on the map, and a new one is without evidence. What data goes to `skill-map` is decided by T07 (architecture 3.3 spec);
  - a lesson begun on an earlier package names the skills of the earlier version (R07): they go to the engine translated by the same rule. What is recorded about a lesson is decided by T12;
  - its own part of the export names, for each module of the log, the version by whose graph the state is computed, and for a module without a current version, that there is no graph and its evidence waits. The log carries the earlier IDs, and the table of an official module a reader will find in the open release of that version (R20 spec; repositories 4 spec). How this goes for third-party modules, of stage 4, is decided together with their licenses (О-10). The export format is epic E4 of `spec` (R76 spec).

— *Why:* the author's decision of 2026-11-02 by the recommendations that the planning of T04 proposed, and by the findings of its independent review. Carrying over at each computation is a direct consequence of R06: the log is the truth, and the state is a cache, which is recomputed when the graph changes. A separate step of carrying over would keep a second place where progress can diverge from the log. The rule "in neither — to no skill" is one for a skill without a pair, for a withdrawn major, for a module without a current version and for any gap in the table. Nothing is lost by this: the log does not change, and a recomputation with a fixed version brings back what waits. A skill without a pair gets nothing, as the format requires, and there is nothing to show it on the map with: there is no title either in `retired_skills` or in the image after a change of the window. `spec` asks for no notice.

— *Rejected:*
- carrying over as a separate step — by a job after the deployment or a migration of the state: R06 decided a lazy recomputation from the log, and carrying over on top of an already computed state R06 rejected;
- rewriting the earlier skill IDs in the log with the new ones: the log is append-only (R06), and the export would lose which skill the evidence was about;
- a skill without a pair on the map with the mark "retired" and the earlier progress: there is no title in `retired_skills`, and the package of the earlier version leaves the image with a change of the window (R07);
- notifying the adult which skills went away: `api` would store per profile which version the learner saw, and `spec` asks for no notice;
- reverse carrying over after a withdrawal of the newest major — routing the evidence of its new skills to the skills of the previous major by `retired_skills` of the withdrawn version: the format has no such rule, a merge cannot be unambiguously split back, and `learner-model` would get two graphs of one module (R28 spec);
- rejecting in CI a pull request after which the previous major becomes current: the harmful version would stay current (R63 spec);
- a check of its own of the coverage of `retired_skills` in CI of `api`: it would repeat the version comparison of `module-kit` (architecture 3.7 spec), and a gap in the table only delays evidence, and the fixed version brings it back.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-02 amendments to R06 — the number of evidence from the log and appending the tail — and to R07 — which graphs `learner-model` gets. What is needed from `learner-model` is В8, and from version comparison in the format, В9 in table 3 of RUN.md; both «в реестр spec». T07 gives the data of the skill map by the IDs of the current version, T12 translates the skills of a lesson on an earlier package. — RUN.md, T04, T07, T09, T12; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2.4, 3, 3.2, 3.3, 3.4 and 4; R06 and R07, the 2026-11-02 amendments.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* an anchor skill `core.<name>` goes as it is if the vocabulary of some format version in the window of `api` knows it, and otherwise to no skill. This is how `spec` recorded the bound of carrying over by В8 in table 3 of RUN.md, specifying that there may be several format versions in the window (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R80 spec). While the format is v0, the window is one version, and the rule comes out the same. The other conditions of carrying over `spec` recorded as `api` asked, and the withdrawn version in version comparison by В9 (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R49 spec). — R16; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.4.

## Showing the solution and hints

**R09. Showing the solution on request (О-7): to a learner of the category `adult` — from the start of the attempt, to a child — from the fourth wrong answer, when the hint ladder has been passed. The setting "ask before showing" in a child's profile is on by default, and then showing is an adult action, and a learning request to show `api` rejects. Showing closes the attempt with the outcome "solution shown".**
- **What was decided** ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.5; sources — section 4):
  - the ladder. A wrong answer is a submission in which at least one answer slot got "not yet"; "unreadable" and "unchecked" do not raise the rung (R04 spec; spec 6.3 canvas-api). The k-th wrong answer gives rung k while k is not more than three, and the attempt stores the number of wrong answers as a whole. From the fourth wrong answer there is no new rung and AI is not called for new words, as `offer_help` in canvas-api: to a child `app` offers to call an adult or to show the solution, to an adult learner — to show the solution. "Explain differently" of rung 3, if it was not asked for yet, remains (R10). This is exactly "after the third rung" of concepts 10 spec;
  - a child is a profile of any age category but `adult` (concepts 13 spec). Showing to them `api` gives only from the fourth wrong answer, by whatever kind of action the request came. A learner of the category `adult` shows the solution from the start of the attempt, by a learning action;
  - the setting "ask before showing" is only in a child's profile, and by default it is on. It is changed by an adult by an action on the profile, `can_manage` (R04). On — `app` asks for the PIN of the section for adults and sends the showing as an adult action, `can_manage`, and a learning request to show `api` rejects with the cause "an adult is needed". Off — showing is a learning action, `can_learn`. The request carries no proof that the PIN was asked for: on a shared device the token is one, and the server tells kinds of actions apart, not people (R04). The refusal is not a security boundary but one place where the setting is enforced for all devices of the account;
  - "call an adult" is an interface of `app`: `api` notifies nobody (architecture 7 spec);
  - showing closes the attempt. The outcome is "solution shown" (concepts 11 spec), that is, the evidence "not solved" (О-7). Showing adds no trap of its own. The traps that the program found in the wrong answers before showing, and the items correct before showing, with their verdict, the evidence carries — this is В11 in table 3 of RUN.md. The task's solution — a closed field — goes to the device when the attempt is closed (R05 spec), the words of hints are deleted in the same transaction (R05), and the segment of the lesson ends ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 21);
  - a correct answer — all items of the task are correct — closes the attempt too, and the solution comes together with the verdict (concepts 6 spec). The tutor's words after a correct answer — praise and a question about which technique helped (concepts 7, 10 spec) — `app` asks for by a second request, like the words of a rung, and `api` gives them on a closed attempt too: once — the attempt marks that they were given, — and while the profile has not begun a new attempt. The words themselves `api` does not store, and this call does not get the previous questions: they were deleted with the words of hints (R05);
  - a task with a rubric has neither a ladder nor showing the solution: an item with a rubric has no verdict (concepts 9.1 spec), and such a task may have no solution ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 14). Its path is decided by T13;
  - the ladder is per task, not per item (concepts 10 spec). So on a workbook page with a dozen items four submissions with an error offer to show the solution of the whole page. This is a consequence of the rule of `spec`, and `api` does not change it;
  - the recommendation of `api` to О-7 is В10 in table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec».

— *Why:* the author's decision of 2026-11-02 by the recommendation that the planning of T05 proposed: showing after the ladder and with an adult's setting, as О-7 recommends. The threshold is the fourth wrong answer, as in canvas-api, where the ladder of concepts 10 spec came from: there the analysis went on only while «неверных ответов на холсте не больше трёх» (R39 canvas-api), and from the fourth `offer_help` came (spec 6.3 canvas-api). So a child first tries the simpler case of the third rung, and there is no AI call beyond three rungs. The setting is on by default: in MathTrail a child did not see the solution before a correct answer at all (R39 canvas-api), and this way an adult sees it first, and the same adult can turn the setting off. Showing closes the attempt, because the key does not go to the device until the task is solved (R05 spec): an answer after a shown solution would check nothing. "Solution shown" means "not solved" — as "I don't know" in standalone: "a child who has seen the solution has not solved the task; counting it as wrong keeps the rating an honest estimate" (R93 standalone).

— *Rejected:*
- never showing the solution to a child, as R39 canvas-api: О-7 does not recommend this;
- offering showing already from the third rung: a child would skip the simpler case;
- a setting off by default, like the switch "Ask before “Show solution”" of the prototype: it is off, but the prototype's code reads its value nowhere;
- showing without closing the attempt: the solution carries the key, and it would be on the device while the attempt is open (R05 spec);
- the adult looks at the solution in their own section, and the child's attempt goes on: the section is on the same device, and the key would be there too;
- a check of the PIN on the server with a short confirmation: R04 rejected it;
- showing to a child before the fourth wrong answer by an adult action: the setting would then mean "showing at any moment", and О-7 puts showing to a child after the ladder;
- "skipped, the rating does not change", as "Show solution" of the prototype: О-7 did not choose this option.

— *Consequence:* the child's profile gets a setting, and the attempt the number of wrong answers, the time of showing and the close reason (R10; the table of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2). The table of actions of section 3.1 gets a row for showing. T07 carries the request to show by two kinds of actions and the refusal "an adult is needed", T12 the end of a segment with showing, T13 the path without a ladder. The recommendation to О-7 is В10, and what showing means for the evidence is В11 in table 3 of RUN.md; both «в реестр spec». — RUN.md, T05, T07, T12, T13; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2.5, 3.1, 3.2 and 3.5.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10* (T37; R47): showing the solution in code. An adult action is checked for the right `can_manage` by the handler: the checks before it hold `can_learn`, as the table of methods does, — without the right, `permission_denied` without `Refusal`. The order of refusals: a task with a rubric — "no showing"; a child before the fourth wrong answer — "ladder not passed", by any kind of action; a learning action with "ask before showing" on — "an adult is needed". The closing "solution shown" deletes the words of hints, and the answer is a solved task, as with "correct"; what comes next is with the engine choice and the lesson (T38, T40).

**R10. A hint on request (О-21): before the first wrong answer — "givens and unknown" and the task's technique, after it — the current rung once more, without AI; "explain differently" — the same rung in other words, one AI call per rung, without the work. The model's question and the fallback text speak of the first wrong answer slot in order. A profile has one open attempt: a new one closes the previous one, and without actions an attempt is closed after 7 days.**
- **What was decided** ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.5; sources — section 4):
  - a request "Hint" before the first wrong answer — and after submissions where there were no wrong ones, only "unreadable" or "unchecked", — gives "givens and unknown" and the task's technique, without AI and without reading the work, and `api` records that the learner saw them (R57 spec). R57 spec says "before the first submission", and before the first wrong answer there is no rung yet — this is the recommendation of `api` to О-21, В10 in table 3 of RUN.md;
  - after a wrong answer the request gives once more the words of the current rung that the attempt stores (R05), — without AI and without the work: `app` sends the request without the work (R69 spec). If there are no words — the second request for them did not arrive, — the learner gets the fallback text of the rung, also without AI, as in canvas-api (spec 6.3 canvas-api). The rung grows only with wrong answers (R03 spec);
  - "explain differently" is the same rung in other words: one AI call per rung, within the quota (T06). The right is spent as soon as the call has gone, however it ended; with an exhausted quota or without an AI route there is no call, and the right stays. A second time on the same rung `api` rejects with a cause, and `app` keeps the earlier words. The model gets the same as the words of a rung by architecture 4.1 spec, but for the work: the key and the solution of the task, the trap of the rung's answer slot, the rung, the presentation style, the explanation language, the age category and the AI route, — and the task's previous questions (privacy 4 spec). The trap — the program's finding by the verdict — the words of a rung get in architecture 4.1 spec too, in the second request, when the verdict is already recorded. The model does not get what was read, just as the words of a rung do not get it, and does not get the work because the request does not send it;
  - the answer to "differently" passes the leak check and the form check (R70 spec). If it did not come or did not pass, the learner gets the fallback text of the rung, and if they are reading exactly that text, the refusal "no other words", without a repeat: in canvas-api the trap text that matched an already shown hint was also replaced by a template (spec 6.3 canvas-api). Without AI — a tapped choice in the mode without AI (О-5) — "explain differently" is not offered;
  - each call of an attempt's words — both of a rung and of "differently" — gets the previous questions of the same task, so as not to repeat them, as the model of R39 canvas-api got «ступень подсказки и прошлые вопросы этого холста». There is no field for them in the request for a hint of `tutor` yet — В10;
  - an AI failure, an exhausted quota and words that did not pass the leak check or the form check give the fallback text of the rung: the verdict is already given, and the learner always gets an answer (concepts 10 spec). A neutral phrase is only for the verdict "unchecked" (R04 spec; architecture 4.1 spec). This is the answer to the question that `tutor` left to the detailing of `api` (architecture 3.7 tutor);
  - the rung's answer slot is the first answer slot "not yet" in order in the submission that gave the rung: items by place, answer slots by order in the item. The program chooses it (R03 spec). Its trap the words of a rung and of "differently" get, and the fallback text is taken by the chain of the format: on rungs 1–2, the text of its trap value, without it the trap advice of its trap, without a trap the rung template, and on the third, the simpler case of its item ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 19). From the fourth wrong answer there is no rung, and the answer slot stays the one that rung 3 speaks about: "Hint", the fallback text and "differently" speak about one place. `api` does not store the answer slot: it is derived from the verdicts of the submission;
  - the evidence. The outcome is by the wrong answers, "after hint number k" (concepts 11 spec). A request and "explain differently" do not change the outcome, and the evidence carries whether the learner saw the technique and "givens and unknown" and how many times they asked for "differently". The weights are decided by `learner-model` — В11;
  - the end of an attempt is a correct answer or showing the solution (R09), "unchecked" on a withdrawn version or outside the window (R07), and also leaving and the time limit:
    - a profile has one open attempt. A new one — by a lesson, by the engine choice or the same task again — closes the previous one with the reason "left", in the same transaction, and a constraint of the database does not let a second open one in, so two devices of one profile do not open two attempts at once; how exactly is the code epic's. So a child who has nobody to call leaves the task, and `app` expects exactly that: the attempt ended also "when the learner has begun the same task anew" (architecture 3.2 app). If the profile has already seen the solution of this task, and it is not a template, the evidence of the new attempt carries that too — the learner knows the key. This is derived from the earlier attempts, and the weight is decided by `learner-model` — В11;
    - an attempt without actions for 7 days is abandoned. It is closed by the check on request and by the operator's daily retention job; the same job is the safety net for the attempts of R07. A closing by the job does not move the profile's activity date (R05);
    - the outcome of a left and of an abandoned attempt: there were wrong answers — the new outcome "left unfinished", and the evidence carries the traps that the program found; there were none — there is no evidence, and the attempt stays a row with a close reason and is visible in progress (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R06). The outcome "left unfinished" concepts 11 spec does not name — В11. The name is not "not solved": that is what О-7 calls the value "solution shown", and one term means one thing (RULES.md, The bar);
  - what an attempt stores beyond the table of section 3.2: the number of wrong answers; for a rung where the learner asked for "differently", that they asked and the source of those words; whether the words after a correct answer were given; the time the solution was shown; the close reason — "correct", "solution shown", "left", "abandoned", "unchecked" — and the time of the last action. All this goes into the export. By which kind of action the solution was shown `api` does not store: the outcome does not need it, and the data is a minimum (privacy 1 spec);
  - the words of hints still live until the end of the attempt (R05), and an abandoned attempt ends no later than 8 days after the last action: 7 days and a day until the job.

— *Why:* the author's decision of 2026-11-02 by the recommendations that the planning of T05 proposed, and by the findings of its independent review. A request by rung and "differently" of the same rung are the recommendation of О-21: if a request raised the rung, the learner would get to the simpler case and to the solution without trying themselves, and the rung is chosen by the program by the wrong answers (R03 spec). "Differently" is once per rung: so beyond the words of the rungs there are no more than three calls per attempt, and the quota itself is T06. Without the work — because the request sends no work (R69 spec), and the model gets the same as the words of a rung, and the previous questions, which privacy 4 spec lets go to AI from what is stored. The first wrong answer slot in order is a rule without AI, one for the model and for the fallback text: both speak about one place. One open attempt — as in MathTrail: in standalone a task without an answer becomes a skip when the next one is asked for (R98 standalone), and in canvas-api a canvas is abandoned only by a new canvas of the same task (spec 9.7 canvas-api). A time limit is needed on top of that: the words of hints live until the end of the attempt (R05), and an abandoned attempt a new one may never close. 7 days — so that the learner can come back to the task after a weekend too. Leaving without wrong answers does not move the model, as the skip of R98 standalone, where punishing a skip would mean "punish a stray tap", and the wrong answers before leaving are evidence, and leaving does not hide them.

— *Rejected:*
- each request raises the rung, as in the author's analysis (О-21) and in the prototype's code: the learner would get to the solution without trying themselves (R03 spec);
- "explain differently" without a limit, within one quota: a child would spend the account's quota by presses;
- no "explain differently": other words of the same rung are what a learner who did not understand the question lacks;
- "differently" with the work of the last submission: `app` sends the request without the work, and the work would have to be kept on the device and sent once more;
- "differently" with what was read from the database instead of the work: of what is stored, privacy 4 spec lets only the previous questions go to AI, and what was read the words of a rung do not get either (architecture 4.1 spec), — an amendment to privacy 4 spec would be needed;
- the outcome of a left attempt "not solved", as the plan of T05 called it: that is what О-7 calls the value "solution shown", and the term would mean two different things;
- an answer slot with a trap text ahead of the first wrong one: the hint would jump around the page;
- the answer slot where the analysis found the first error: the choice would depend on AI, and without an analysis the rule would be different;
- a neutral phrase on an AI failure on words: the verdict is already given, and the fallback text of the rung helps more;
- an abandoned attempt only by a time limit, of 24 hours: a child who came back the next evening would lose the attempt, and leaving the task earlier would be impossible;
- the outcome "unchecked" for a left and an abandoned attempt: if `learner-model` gives it zero weight, the wrong answers before leaving will be lost to the model;
- leaving without wrong answers as a wrong answer: R98 standalone rejected the same.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-02 amendment to R06 — there is no evidence for an attempt closed by leaving or by the time limit without wrong answers. In the table of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2, are the new fields of an attempt and the lifetime of the words of hints of an abandoned attempt. T06 counts "differently" in the quota; T07 carries the request, "differently" and their refusals, the words after a correct answer on a closed attempt and the closing of the previous attempt by a new one; T08 the close reason in the export; T12 what is recorded about a lesson that was left. The recommendations to О-21 and the conditions for `tutor` are В10, and those for concepts 11 spec, `learner-model` and `engine` are В11 in table 3 of RUN.md; both «в реестр spec». — RUN.md, T05, T06, T07, T08, T12; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2.5, 3.2 and 3.5; R05, R06.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* a new close reason of an attempt — "not suitable": the lesson run ended "not suitable" while its attempt was open (R13). The evidence is as for "left": there were wrong answers — "left unfinished", there were none — there is no evidence, as the 2026-11-02 amendment to R06 decided for leaving and the time limit. Open attempts outside a lesson are still not rechecked: R51 spec checks the task at the start of an attempt, and such an attempt brings no new tasks. Not "left" — that is the name of the profile's leaving for another attempt, and the term would get two meanings (RULES.md, The bar). The author's decision in the planning of T12; what this means for the evidence goes to В11 in table 3 of RUN.md. — R13; RUN.md, T12; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2 and 3.5.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* a new close reason of an attempt — "done": the learner finished an attempt of a task with a rubric (R14). It is accepted after at least one submission of the attempt, and there is no evidence, as for any attempt with a rubric (О-17; R06). A left and an abandoned attempt with a rubric give no evidence either: they have no wrong answers. Not "correct" — an item with a rubric has no verdict (concepts 9.1 spec), — and not "left": that is the name of the profile's leaving for another attempt. The author's decision in the planning of T13; what this means for the evidence goes to В11 in table 3 of RUN.md. — R14; RUN.md, T13; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2, 3.5 and 3.8.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* О-7 and О-21 were closed by R82 spec by the recommendations of `api` — В10 and В11 in table 3 of RUN.md, — with refinements of the review that change this entry:
- that the learner saw "givens and unknown" `api` does not record, and the evidence does not carry it. They are open, and in the solving mode "by steps" `app` shows them at a step without a request to `api`, so a record would mean something other than what it says (R82 spec). On request they are shown by `app` too — from the task's open view;
- the task's technique `api` gives and records, at a step or on request. At a step whose `gives` gives techniques, `app` names the attempt and the step's place, and `api` answers with the IDs of the task's techniques, empty if there are none; a request before the first wrong answer gets the same (architecture 3.5 spec). Which technique was given and how, the evidence carries;
- the evidence carries whether the solution of this task went to the profile's device earlier — after a correct answer or showing, and not only after showing: the learner sees the solution after a correct answer too (concepts 6 spec). A template has no such fact: a new attempt gives new numbers;
- the outcome "left unfinished" is recorded in concepts 11 spec, and the task's previous questions in each words call `tutor` gets by its own move to a tag (R82 spec).

How this goes in the protocol — the request, the start of a step and their answers — is decided by T07. — R16; R82 spec; RUN.md, T07; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2 and 3.5.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* in the protocol "no other words" is an outcome in a successful answer, not a refusal: the call was made, and the right to "differently" is spent. This entry called a refusal the fact that there are no new words. "Already asked" is a refusal: there is no call (R17). — R17; RUN.md, T07; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.5 and 3.9.

— *Amendment 2026-11-09* (T30; R40): from format 0.4 "Hint" before the first wrong answer gives, beside the IDs of the techniques, also the leading question of the attempt's instance, `leading_question`, if the task has one: the field `GetHintResponse.leading_question`, and an open attempt holds the one given in `OpenAttempt.requested_leading_question` (R108 spec; the 2026-11-08 amendment to R82 spec; [contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15). Beside the words of a rung the question is always empty. `api` records that it gave the question only when it is not empty, and the question goes into the evidence only by its own field of `learner-model` — after its release with this field, and not by the field of the technique on request: the log is append-only, and the recomputation would no longer separate mixed facts (the 2026-11-09 amendment to R82 spec; Д138 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`). The code is T34 and T37, and the field in the evidence comes after the release of `learner-model` (Д17 in table 4 of RUN.md).

— *Amendment 2026-11-10* (T33; R43): one open attempt of a profile is held by the database — the unique partial index `attempts (profile_id) WHERE closed_at IS NULL`, — and the close reason and time are set together. An abandoned attempt — 7 days without actions — is closed by the step `abandoned_attempts` of the job `api sweep`: `UPDATE` in batches of 1000, past the rows that a request holds, with the time limit checked once more on the row itself, and without the profile's activity date. The words of hints it will delete when T37 introduces them, and the evidence "left unfinished" when T39 introduces it: until then no closing writes evidence.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10* (T34; R44): a new attempt closes the open one in the same transaction that begins it: "left", and an overdue one "unchecked" or "abandoned", as the check on request would close it. "Abandoned" is also closed by the check on request: a request on an attempt without actions for 7 days closes it, and the closing stays, and the request itself gets "attempt closed" with the reason "abandoned". An action on an attempt is its start, a submission and the start of a step. Reading the open attempt is not an action and does not move the last action: opening an attempt on another device is not yet work on the task. The number of wrong answers and the time when the task's technique was first given at a step are columns of the attempt, the migration `0006`.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10* (T37; R47): in code the words of hints are the table `attempt_rungs` of the migration `0008`, a row per rung of the attempt: the source and the words, and after "explain differently", its time, source and the words that the learner reads from then on. The words are deleted by a database trigger at the closing of an attempt — by any reason, including by the step `abandoned_attempts` of the job `api sweep`, — in the transaction of the closing; the rung number and the source stay (R05). "Hint" before the first wrong answer is an action on the attempt: it moves the time of the last action, as the start of a step does (the 2026-11-10 amendment to this entry, T34). That the techniques and the leading question were given on request the attempt writes by two times — `requested_techniques_at` and `leading_question_at`, each only when the task has it, — so the evidence of T39 will take them separately (Д138 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`).

— *Amendment 2026-11-10* (T39; R49): the evidence in code carries the technique at a step and on request — by `step_techniques_at` and `requested_techniques_at`, — how many times the learner asked for "explain differently", by the rungs where they asked, and whether the solution of the task — not of a template — went to the device earlier: by the earlier attempts of the same task, closed "correct" or "solution shown" before the start of this one. There is no leading question in it: `learner-model` v0.1.4 has no field for it (Д17). The outcome "after hint number k" is the rung that the wrong answers reached, `min(wrong, 3)`; the outcome "with a sketch hint" `api` does not write — there is no such hint in it.

## Platform AI quota

**R11. The platform AI quota is dollars by the estimate of `tutor`, per family day, with two caps: the profile's — $0.50, the account's — $1.50, as the first values of the tariff "closed slice". The account gets the tariff with the invite code. An exhausted quota closes platform AI until the next day: a check of handwriting is "unchecked", words are the fallback text, and the attempt waits.**
- **What was decided** ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.6; sources — section 4):
  - the unit is dollars by the estimate from the spending message of `tutor`: the model, the tokens and the price by its price table with a date (architecture 3.7 tutor). So the card requires: "platform AI quotas — by the spending that `tutor` reports" (architecture 3.5 spec). The quota is only on platform AI: the spending of an own key the platform does not accumulate, and the limit is set by the key's owner (concepts 14 spec; R55 tutor). There is no automatic move from an own key to platform AI: it is another AI route and another processor (privacy 5.1, 5.2 spec);
  - each model call on platform AI is counted: both readers of handwriting (R68 spec), the words of a rung, "explain differently" (R10), the words after a correct answer (R09), the reading and the assessment of an essay (R75 spec);
  - there are two caps, per day, as in canvas-api (spec 14.5 canvas-api). The profile's cap is so that the children of one account do not share the quota "first come, first served", the account's cap is a cap on spending. The first values are the profile $0.50, the account $1.50: in canvas-api the cap of the worst day was ≈ $0.52 per child and ≈ $1.55 per parent. These are values of the tariff's configuration, and a pull request of the configuration changes them, not an amendment to this entry. The owner profile spends the same cap of the account, and an account with one profile runs into the profile's cap;
  - the day is the family's, as R293 standalone. `app` sends the offset of its clock from UTC with the requests that call AI, the account holds the offset and the end of the current day, and a new offset takes effect from the next day. A day runs from the family's midnight to midnight, and the first day by a new offset ends at the first midnight by it that comes no earlier than 20 hours after the end of the previous day: otherwise a change of the offset from +14 hours to −12 would give a day of 2 hours and a second quota. So after a trip east by more than 4 hours, the first day by the new offset is longer — up to 44 hours. An offset outside −12…+14 hours or not a multiple of a quarter of an hour `api` rejects. Until a device has reported an offset, the day is UTC. Summer time and a trip are an ordinary change of the offset;
  - the tariff is a field of the account: privacy 4 spec names it in the row "Account". The operator creates an invite code with a tariff — by default "closed slice", — and at the redemption of the code the tariff passes to the account (R04). The tariff is changed by an operator's command that finds the account by its code: the account ID does not leave the platform. The caps of each tariff are in the configuration of `api`. If the account has no tariff or the tariff has no caps, platform AI is closed: "closed by default", as with the admission function (architecture 3.9 tutor);
  - the quota is step 7 of the checks of section 3.1, only for requests that call AI. It changes what the learner gets, and does not reject the request;
  - exhausted — the profile's or the account's cap does not cover the request's reserve (R12). Then there is no call:
    - a check of handwriting and of a choice written by hand — "unchecked" and a neutral phrase (R04 spec), and the rung does not grow (R09);
    - the words of a rung — the fallback text, and "differently" is not called, and the right to it stays (R10);
    - there are no words after a correct answer;
    - an essay is not read, and what the learner sees is decided by T13;
    - a tapped choice is checked as always (architecture 4.2 spec).

    The refusal carries the cause "quota" and the time when the next day begins. The text is in `app`, and it names no numbers, as SPEC 10.3 standalone, but it may promise "tomorrow": the day is the family's;
  - in the middle of a task the attempt waits. It is open until a new attempt or the limit of 7 days closes it (R10), and `app` says that checking will come back tomorrow. A new task with handwriting `api` begins only if the remainder covers a typical task — two checks, words and praise: this is a value of the configuration. While the attempt waits, `api` does not offer a new task by itself: the engine is called only when the learner asks for another task. If they asked, the waiting attempt is closed with the reason "left", as always, the wrong answers before leaving give "left unfinished" (R10), and `app` warns that there is no coming back to it. A lesson's segment waits the same way, and what is recorded about the lesson is decided by T12;
  - AI closed for a new task — one rule for a quota that does not cover a typical task, the admission refusal "not admitted" (architecture 3.9 tutor) and the provider's refusal by a limit: `api` gives the engine only tasks that are checked without AI, as R57 tutor requires for admission. In the first slice there are almost no such tasks (product 10 spec), and then the learner sees that there is no checking until tomorrow, and on the provider's refusal, that there is none for now. What this requires from `engine` is В14 in table 3 of RUN.md;
  - the provider's refusal by a limit on the platform's key is the outcome "limit reached" of `tutor` (architecture 3.7 tutor). The provider's limit is monthly, and such a refusal does not promise "tomorrow": its cause is "platform limit". `api` closes platform AI for everyone until an operator's command opens it again, and writes the event code into the log, without an ID: this is a signal to the operator. The spending limit of the workspace at the provider is set by the author (rule 4 of RUN.md; Д11 in table 4 of RUN.md);
  - the adult sees in their section the tariff, the share of today's quota of the profile and the account and when the next day begins, not dollars. The request is T07, the view is `app`'s;
  - stage 3 adds tariffs and payment (architecture 3.13 spec; О-14), and the rule does not change: the caps are set by the account's tariff. The caps of stage 2 are not a model for stage 3 ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.6) — В12 in table 3 of RUN.md;
  - the first app does not require the quota: the author's calls go by the route "own key" (architecture 3.9 tutor), and tariffs are not part of the first app (`COORDINATION.md` of `spec`). The quota is needed before platform AI opens to anyone but the author — Д11.

— *Why:* the author's decision of 2026-11-02 by the recommendations that the planning of T06 proposed, and by the findings of its independent review. Dollars — because a reading call and an analysis call cost differently, and so do the tokens of different models. By this argument `tutor` chose an estimate in dollars for the spending message: «С оценкой в долларах `api` считает квоты и юнит-экономику (О-14), не зная цен моделей» (R55 tutor). Two caps — as in canvas-api, where the parent's limits moreover survived the deletion of a child (spec 14.5 canvas-api). The family day is a lesson of standalone: a UTC day ended for the author "at seven in the evening" and broke off a session (R293 standalone). The least length of a day is needed because R293 standalone defers a new offset until the next day but does not bound its length. The tariff from the code — because the code is the only way into the closed slice anyway (R04), and privacy 4 spec holds the tariff in the account anyway. The attempt waits — because a left one with wrong answers gives "left unfinished" anyway, and a new reason or "unchecked" would hide the wrong answers from the learner model, as R10 rejected for leaving.

— *Rejected:*
- calls by kind, as the counters of readings and analyses of canvas-api: two readers and a change of model change the price of a call, and the card requires counting by spending;
- "checks" as the unit: a check of handwriting and a check of an essay cost differently;
- only the account's cap: the children of one account would share the quota, first come, first served;
- a UTC day, as spec 11 canvas-api: for a family in the USA the quota would renew at 19–20 hours, and the phrase could not promise "tomorrow";
- a sliding day: "tomorrow" would become the hour when the day began (R293 standalone);
- a time zone that the adult chooses: an extra step, and until it is made, the day is UTC;
- one tariff for everyone and a tariff only from stage 3: the author and the testers need their own cap, and the rule of the quota would have to be tied to the tariff again;
- an operator's command by the account ID: the ID would leave the platform (R04);
- closing the attempt with the reason "quota" without evidence or with the outcome "unchecked": the wrong answers before it would be lost to the learner model (R10);
- an automatic move from an own key to platform AI: another processor and an unnoticed spending of the quota.

— *Consequence:* step 7 of section 3.1 is decided. The account gets the tariff, the offset and the end of the day, and the invite code the tariff (R12; the table of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2). T07 carries the request for the quota, the causes "quota", "platform limit" and "not admitted" with the time of the next day, and the offset; T12 the lesson's segment that waits; T13 an essay with an exhausted quota. The recommendation to О-14 is В12, the conditions for `tutor` В13, for `engine` В14 in table 3 of RUN.md, all «в реестр spec». Stage 3 is Д10, the quota in code is Д11 in table 4. — RUN.md, T06, T07, T12, T13; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2.6, 3.1, 3.2, 3.5 and 3.6.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* a task with a rubric has its own typical task — two readings of the essay with an assessment, the first submission and one rewrite: ≈ $0.06 by the price of an essay in `tutor`, $0.030 with Opus 5.5 · `high` (architecture 3.4 tutor). This is a value of the configuration of its own: two checks of handwriting, words and praise do not fit an essay. An exhausted quota on an essay is "unchecked" and a neutral phrase, and the attempt waits, like any: R14 decided this. The author's decision together with the plan of T13. — R14; RUN.md, T13; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.6 and 3.8.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* "the author's calls go by the route 'own key'" in the first app diverges from the cross-repository plan of `spec`, and it was so already on v0.6.2: an own key is not part of the first app, and the work is read by Claude models with a key from an environment variable (`COORDINATION.md` of `spec`, «Первое приложение»; R76 spec). The author's calls in the first app go by the route of platform AI, with a key from an environment variable on their machine, and there is no quota in code yet — Д11 in table 4 of RUN.md: the counters and the tariff in code come before platform AI opens to anyone but the author. The rule of the quota this does not change. To В12–В14 `spec` answered with additions to О-14 and П-9, concepts 12, 14 and 15 spec, privacy 4 spec, the cards architecture 3.5, 3.6 and 3.8 spec and a new question П-23 — whether the device's clock offset falls under "no geolocation" (R16). — R16; RUN.md, Д11; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1 and 3.6.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* a "refusal" with the cause "quota" for a request that this entry does not reject is, in the protocol, an outcome in a successful answer: the verdict "unchecked" or the fallback text with the cause "quota" and the time of the next day. A refusal — an error of the protocol — is for the quota at the start of an attempt and of a lesson's segment, and for "explain differently", which without a reserve does not call AI (R17). The request for the quota for the section for adults T07 left to epic E4 of `spec`, together with consent: this is what comes «с детьми и тарифами», and the first app does not need it (R76 spec). The author's decision in the planning of T07. — R17; RUN.md, T07; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.6 and 3.9.

— *Amendment 2026-11-07:* the clock offset `app` sends only with requests to platform AI, and with an own key it does not: so the contract decided by the recommendation of `api`, by the author's answer to row В36 (R97 spec; П-23). "With the requests that call AI" above means "with requests to platform AI". `BeginAttempt` and "something else" do not call AI and carry no offset: the day that their refusal "quota" names is the one that `api` holds with the account. — R23; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.6 and 3.9.

— *Amendment 2026-11-08:* the estimate of spending in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.6, is by the price of the words of a rung from the measurement T76 of `tutor`, $0.0152 per call with Opus 5.5 · `high` (architecture 3.4 tutor), and not $0.0098 of the series T40 — the answer of a judge with the key and the analysis. The solving mode "self" — 20 tasks, 30% with one error, 26 checks and 26 words calls — costs ≈ 26 × ($0.0086 + $0.0152) ≈ $0.62, "by steps" the same, and the profile's cap of $0.50 covers about 16 tasks, not 20. So the section agrees with О-14, which `spec` has already fixed (R23). The caps do not change: they are values of the tariff's configuration, and the estimate is a guide for them and for О-14. *Why:* the author's answer of 2026-11-08 to В28, by its recommendation — option (a). *Rejected:* fixing it with a code task that touches section 3.6 — until then the section would diverge from О-14, on which the tariffs of stage 3 stand; leaving it as a guide — the numbers are wrong, not rough. — RUN.md, T16; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.6 and 4.

— *Amendment 2026-11-12* (T61; R62): the request for the quota that T07 left to epic E4 of `spec` was recorded by the contract of `spec` v0.16.3 — `AccountService.GetQuota` (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R98 spec; T143 spec). A learning action with `can_learn` gets the share of its own profile, and an adult action with `can_view` also the share of the account: whole percent rounded down, by the settled spending of the day, without the reserves of running calls, with the mark "cap reached" and the start of the next day; a tariff without caps has no shares. Beyond this entry, the share of their own profile is seen by the learner too — where they begin work, — and the share of the account a child never sees. In code the method will come with epic E9 of RUN.md, and until then it answers `unimplemented` (R62).

— *Amendment 2026-11-13* (T70; the 2026-11-12 amendment to R98 spec; Д229 spec): `GetQuota` is in the code, `internal/account`, and no longer answers `unimplemented`.
- **The right.** `ACTION_UNSPECIFIED` is `invalid_argument`. A learning action goes by `rpc.Learner`, with its refusals of consent and of age. An adult's asks `can_view` in the handler, as the skill map does, and without it gets `permission_denied` with no `Refusal`, as the contract has it for a right that is missing; it asks for no consent of the learner.
- **The reading.** `store.QuotaSettled` reads in one snapshot and writes nothing: the family day the account keeps at the time of the request, by `store.Today` — the request carries no offset (R56) —, the caps of the tariff, and what the counters of the profile and of the account of that day have spent, without their reserve (R12, R57). A day with no counter has spent nothing, and a profile not of the account is "no profile".
- **The share.** `quota.Share` — whole percent rounded down, 100 at the cap and past it. `cap_reached` is the settled spending of the profile or of the account at its cap, beside a learning action too, which gets no share of the account. A tariff without caps, or no tariff, gives neither share and `cap_reached` false. `next_family_day` is the end of that day.
- **Rejected:** counting the reserves in, as `store.QuotaFits` does — the contract has the share grow only within a day, and a reserve returned would lower it; writing the day of a new offset, as `store.FamilyDay` — the request carries none, and a read that writes would hold the account's row; the refusal "no profile" for an adult without `can_view`, as the export and the storage of works give it for `can_manage` — the contract and the skill map give `permission_denied`.

**R12. The quota is counted by a reserve before a call and a settlement by the spending message in the day of the reserve; a broken-off call is counted as the reserve, not as the worst case. The counters of the profile and the account live no longer than 2 days after the end of their own day; deleting a profile takes its counters away, and the account's counters stay.**
- **What was decided** ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.6; sources — section 4):
  - the reserve is taken before each request that calls AI, on the counters of the profile and the account in one transaction. The request goes if on each of them what is spent, what is in reserve and the new reserve together are no more than the cap. So two devices of one account do not slip past the cap at once, and a reserve at the start of a check was in R35 canvas-api too;
  - the size of the reserve is the expected price of the kind of call, not the worst case. The worst case of one answer in `tutor` is 4,000 tokens of input and 16,000 of output (architecture 3.3 tutor), for Opus 5.5 this is about $0.34, and a reserve by it would close checks long before the cap. The expected price of a kind by its price table and the model of the kind is given by `tutor` — a condition for it, В13; until then, a value by kind in the configuration of `api`. A check of handwriting reserves both readers together, an essay the reading together with the assessment, a workbook page by the number of crops: the quota does not run out in the middle of a check;
  - the settlement is in the day when the reserve was taken:
    - an exact spending record is counted at its own price;
    - a record by the worst case — for an answer cut off before the tokens were counted (architecture 3.7 tutor) — is counted as the reserve: a failure of the platform does not eat a child's day (R04 spec). The worst case goes into the spending log;
    - there was no call — the reserve is returned;
    - the provider refused one reader by a limit — only the record of the one that answered is counted (architecture 3.7 tutor);
    - there is no spending message at all — the reserve stays;
    - the process crashed — the reserve stays as the spending of its day, as in R35 canvas-api a call that was made was counted even on a failure. Settling the running requests when an instance stops is the code epic;
  - overspending beyond the cap is no more than the difference between the exact price and the reserve for each running request. It is held by the limited `MaxOutputTokens` of each kind of product call — a condition for `tutor`, В13;
  - what is stored: the profile's counter and the account's counter — the start of the day, spent and in reserve; in the account — the tariff, the offset and the end of the current day; in the invite code — the tariff; the flag "platform AI closed" — one per deployment. The counters are keyed by the start of the day, not by a date: with a change of the offset the family's date may repeat;
  - retention periods: a counter is needed for its own day and for one more day after its end — for settling requests across midnight, — and then the operator's daily retention job deletes it. So a counter lives no longer than 2 days after the end of its own day, and leaves the history of Neon a day later still (R05). A counter is created with the first reserve of the day, so a day without requests has none. This is beyond the recommendation of П-9 — В12 in table 3 of RUN.md;
  - the export is the tariff, the offset, the end of the current day and all stored counters: everything goes into the export but secrets and service fields (R05);
  - deleting a profile takes its counters away, and the account's counters stay: otherwise a deletion and a new profile would lift the cap on spending (spec 9.8 canvas-api). Deleting the account takes everything away, and a new account needs a new one-time code (R04), so the cap is not lifted then either. With the open sign-in of stage 3 this stops holding — Д10 in table 4 of RUN.md;
  - logs — without IDs (R09 spec). In them are the spending message of `tutor`, which has neither content nor identifiers (architecture 3.7 tutor), and the event codes "quota exhausted" and "platform limit". The offset is in no line of the log, as in R293 standalone. Unit economics reads sums by kind of call and model and the number of exhaustions per day, and `api` writes no summaries by account;
  - the check against privacy spec: the counters and the offset are pseudonymous data about when and how much a family learns (privacy 1 spec), and they do not go to AI. The offset is a rough place, and privacy 1 spec promises that there is no geolocation. Whether the offset falls under this promise is not verified: this is a question for `spec`, В12. The new fields of the account add to В3 — the register of the fields of `api`, the table of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2.

— *Why:* the author's decision of 2026-11-02 together with the plan of T06. The reserve and a call that was made being counted — as R35 canvas-api: «Сделанный вызов засчитывается, даже если он не удался или вышел по тайм-ауту: он может быть оплачен, а дневной лимит должен оставаться потолком трат». The provider does indeed charge for a broken-off request: "If your client disconnects or times out in the middle of a request that was on track to succeed, that request is still charged" (section 4). But the price of such a request `tutor` knows only by the worst case, and the quota counts the reserve so that a failure does not punish the learner (R04 spec). The account's counters survive the deletion of a profile, as spec 9.8 canvas-api. The period is shorter than the 30 days of canvas-api: the quota needs only its own day and one more day after it — for settling requests across midnight, — and the data must be as little as possible (privacy 1 spec). There are no summaries by account: with a handful of participants of the closed slice they are personal data.

— *Rejected:*
- a reserve by the worst case: with Opus about $0.34 per call, and the profile's cap would run out in one and a half reserves;
- counting a broken-off call at its worst case: a failure of the platform would eat a child's day;
- returning the reserve of a crashed request: its calls may have been paid for (R35 canvas-api);
- counters for 30 days, as in canvas-api: the quota does not need them;
- summaries by account for unit economics: with a handful of participants this is personal data, and sums without IDs are enough;
- deleting the account's counters together with the profile: a deletion and a new profile would lift the cap (spec 9.8 canvas-api).

— *Consequence:* in the table of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2, are the new fields of the account and the row "quota counters". The tariff of an invite code and the flag "platform AI closed" carry no data of the learner or the owner, and they are not in the table: codes are not personal data (R29 canvas-api), and the flag is one per deployment. T08 deletes the profile's counters with the profile, and the account's counters with the account, and exports the tariff, the offset and the counters. The conditions for `tutor` are В13, the period beyond П-9 and the offset В12 in table 3 of RUN.md; the quota in code is Д11. — RUN.md, T06, T08; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2 and 3.6; R05.

## Lesson

**R13. `api` keeps a lesson by a lesson run — a row of the database per profile and lesson, on the package the lesson began on, one for all devices. A run is begun by `app`'s request for the lesson that the engine chose, and the learner's leaving for another task does not end it. It ends "passed" — by "lesson passed" from `app`, when all task segments have ended —, and also "unchecked", "not suitable" and "abandoned" after 30 days without actions. The place among the explanations and examples and the state of the demo are held by `app`, and `api` does not know them.**
- **What was decided** ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7; sources — section 4):
  - a lesson run is a row of `api`'s database: the run ID, the profile ID, the lesson's full ID, the module ID and its version, the package digest, the engine choice that gave the lesson, the time of the beginning and of the last action, the end reason and time. The beginning, the end reason and the end time, once recorded, do not change. The term is the same as `app`'s (R06 app). The attempt of a lesson's task carries the run ID and the segment place, from 1. An open run is one per profile and lesson, and a database constraint holds this, and a second simultaneous request rereads the row and gives the open one; how exactly is for the epic of code. There may be several open runs of different lessons;
  - the beginning. A run is begun by `app`'s request for the open view of the lesson that the engine choice named, and the choice itself begins nothing. If the lesson has an open run, `api` gives it. A new one begins on the package of the current version, if the module is connected and it has a current version that suits the profile (R07), the lesson is in it, and the lesson and each of its tasks suit the profile's age category (R58 spec and its 2026-10-28 amendment). There is no other way to a new run: the engine chooses a lesson or a task, and `app` has no list of lessons (R06 app);
  - the answer is the run ID, the lesson's open view whole, the package digest, the places of the task segments that have ended, and the segment's open attempt, if there is one. `api` gives an open run again by its ID — after a restart and on another device —, and `app` gets the profile's open runs and its open attempt with the profile: so a device without the state of the lesson finds them too. A request for a lesson without an open run does not begin a new one: the refusal names how the last one ended;
  - the check is full, every time `api` gives a lesson and begins a segment's attempt: the module — by the current version (R07), and the lesson and each of its tasks — by the run's package, the one the learner sees (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R07). If it fails, the run ends "not suitable", and its open attempt is closed with the reason "not suitable" (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R10). A lesson that does not suit a new run, or that the current version does not have, is a refusal without the open view, and the engine does not get such lessons at all. "Lesson passed" does not serve as a check: it brings nothing to the device, and the run records what was;
  - the task segment. A segment's attempt begins only if the run is open, every task segment before it has ended, and the segment itself has not; an open attempt of the segment is given back the same. It is an attempt on the run's package, with its own seed, the same as outside a lesson (R58 spec): a new one closes another open one with the reason "left" (R10), and a new attempt of the same segment is a new seed, and for a template perhaps another instance (architecture 3.8 spec; R60 spec). The quota and closed AI concern only a task that needs platform AI: if what is left is not enough for a typical task, the refusal "quota" with the time of the next day; if AI is closed, a refusal with a cause, and the run stays open (R11);
  - a segment has ended when its attempt is closed "correct" or "solution shown" (R09; [format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 21). "Left" and "abandoned" do not end it — on return the segment begins a new attempt —, and neither does the submission verdict "unchecked": the attempt waits (R11). The close reason "unchecked" — the version is withdrawn or outside the window (R07) — ends the run "unchecked" in the same transaction. The segment of a task with a rubric ends with its attempt, and how that one ends T13 decides: until then such a segment does not end, the engine does not get a lesson with such a segment, and `api` does not begin a new run of it. At the end of a segment `api` answers what next: the place of the next segment or "no more segments";
  - the run end reasons:
    - "passed" — `app` sent "lesson passed" with the run ID, and all task segments have ended. Before that, a refusal with the places of the segments that have ended. If the run has already ended, the answer names the reason, and for "passed" this is not an error: "lesson passed" may come twice too. The package check on request (R07) goes first, so on a withdrawn version the run ends "unchecked";
    - "unchecked" — a lesson's attempt was closed with this reason, or the job after a deployment and the check on request found a run on a package that the image no longer gives (R07);
    - "not suitable" — the full check failed;
    - "abandoned" — 30 days without actions. An action is any request about the run and its attempts: the lesson's open view, a segment's attempt and its requests, "lesson passed"; closing by a job does not count as an action. The check on request and the operator's daily retention job close it, and the closing does not move the profile's activity date (R05);
    - "early" — a place for an early end of a lesson by the engine. The detailing of `engine` introduces it (architecture 3.8 spec), and until then `api` does not end a lesson early;
  - the engine. If a run ended by a request, `api` calls the engine for the next one, and if it ended by a job, the engine is called when the learner asks. At the end of a segment the engine is not called in the first slice: segments go in the author's order (concepts 5 spec), and there is no consolidation and no early end yet. What the engine needs about lessons is В15 in table 3 of RUN.md;
  - the module version: an open run goes on its own package, and the lesson's skills go to the engine from this package, mapped by `retired_skills` of the current version (R07, R08). A lesson whose run has ended is, by a new choice, a new run on the current version;
  - if the quota ran out mid-lesson, the waiting is not recorded separately: the run is open, and the segment's attempt waits or has not begun. A request for another task closes only the attempt — "left" (R10, R11) —, and the next day the lesson continues. Beginning a run needs no AI: explanations and examples are read without it;
  - if the module was disconnected with an open run, the run ends by the general rules, and the engine does not give it;
  - `api` does not know the place among the explanations and examples and the state of the demo: `app` holds them and after a break returns the learner to where they were (the second 2026-11-01 amendment to R36 spec; R06 app). A worked example is not evidence (R58 spec), and data are kept to a minimum (privacy 1 spec). This is the answer to the question of T12 about the state of the demo of a worked example;
  - a run has no evidence of its own: a segment's attempt gives evidence, as outside a lesson (R06, R10). Whether the weight of evidence needs the fact "in a lesson" is for `learner-model` to decide — to В11 in table 3 of RUN.md;
  - in the table of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2, the row "passed lessons" becomes the row "lesson runs": the term — while the profile lives (П-9), into the export — all the fields, deletion — with the profile (T08).
