An attempt also carries the run ID and the segment place. Neither the ID nor the lesson is written into the logs (R09 spec), and nothing of a run goes to AI: a lesson adds no call of `tutor`;
  - what is needed from `engine` is В15, and from the contract `app` ↔ `api`, from `app` and from concepts 15 spec — В16 in table 3 of RUN.md; both «в реестр spec». privacy 4 spec has no row for a lesson — to В3: `api`'s register of fields is the table of section 3.2.

— *Why:* the author's decision of 2026-11-02 by the recommendations that the planning of T12 proposed, and by the findings of its independent review.
- **A run outlives leaving.** `app` keeps the place and the state of the demo by the key "lesson ID, package digest" and, when `api` gives the lesson again on the same package, opens it at that place (R06 app). A run that ended by leaving would take from the learner the lesson's tasks they have already solved — because of one task on the side or a quota that ran out in the evening. One run for all devices — because the lesson's attempts are at `api` anyway, and another device opens the lesson after the last segment that has ended (R06 app).
- **"Lesson passed" from `app`.** By the end of the last task, a lesson after which an explanation comes, as `gaps-and-posts.yaml` and `first-sentences.yaml`, would end unread, and a lesson without tasks, as `reading-model-essays.yaml`, would never end (`spec/format/examples/lesson/valid/`). `app` decided so too, and `api` matches "lesson passed" against its attempts: only the server ends a task segment ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 21).
- **30 days.** That long `app` keeps the state of a lesson without changes (section 4). A lesson is short — the lesson on its own package rests on that (R07) —, and a run without a term would go on a version months old, while the engine and the adult would see "in progress" lessons that will not be returned to. An attempt's term is 7 days, because the words of hints live with it (R05, R10), while a run holds no content, and a week's break does not cut a lesson off.
- **"Not suitable".** A lesson goes only to a learner whom both the lesson and each of its tasks suit: "Otherwise a task only for adults (П-20) would reach a child in a lesson where nothing is skipped" (R58 spec). The category is confirmed once a year, and when the country changes, the age is given again (concepts 13 spec), so a lesson's task may stop suiting mid-lesson. Check every time a lesson is given and a segment begins — because the lesson's tasks lie ahead.
- **Beginning by request and the run ID.** The engine chooses after each attempt (architecture 3.5 spec), and a choice that the learner did not open must not leave an open run. A new run on the same digest — after "passed" on one device, "abandoned" or "not suitable" — `app`, by the single key "lesson ID, package digest", would open at the place of the former one, past task segments that have not ended in the new one, and it does not open a task segment again (R06 app): the learner would be stuck. The run ID tells them apart — В16.
- **No call of the engine at a segment.** The engine does not reorder segments (concepts 5 spec), and in the first slice a call at each segment would decide nothing. Consolidation and the early end are its detailing (architecture 3.8 spec), and a run has a place for them.

— *Rejected:*
- one open run per profile, which leaving for another task or lesson ends "left": it diverges from R06 app, and the learner would lose the lesson's tasks they have already solved;
- a run without a term: open runs would pile up, a lesson would go on a version months old, and the format window changes rarely;
- a term of 7 days, as for an attempt: it is shorter than the term of the state of a lesson in `app`, and a week's break would cut a lesson off;
- "passed" that `api` infers from the end of the last task, and the place in the lesson at `api`: `app` rejected both (R06 app) — the arguments are above, and the place at `api` would also be a request and a row of the learner's data on each "Continue";
- checking whether a lesson suits only at its beginning: a task only for adults would reach a child, against R58 spec;
- closing the open attempt of a lesson that stopped suiting with the reason "left": that is the name of the profile's leaving for another attempt;
- beginning a run by the engine choice itself: each choice that was not opened would stay an open run;
- a run without an ID, by `app`'s key: a new run on the same digest would open at someone else's place;
- calling the engine after each attempt of a lesson so that it answers "next segment": a call without a decision;
- asking the format that a published lesson ID name no other lesson, as for a trap: the format promises this for skills, traps and anchor skills ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), sections 2, 12 and 16), while a task's key changes under the same ID with a major version (concepts 3 spec), and a run holds the digest of its package anyway.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-02 amendments to R06 — the time of the choice —, to R07 — the check on request during a deployment and the lesson of an open run by its package — and to R10 — the reason "not suitable". What R05, R07, R08 and R11 left to T12 is decided here. T07 carries the requests of the lesson and the run, T08 deletes and exports runs, T13 decides the end of an attempt with a rubric, and with it the end of its segment in a lesson. «В реестр spec» — В15 and В16 and additions to В3, В4 and В11 in table 3 of RUN.md. — RUN.md, T07, T08, T09, T12, T13; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2.7, 2.9, 3, 3.1–3.7 and 4; R06, R07, R10.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* the segment of a task with a rubric ends when its attempt is closed "done" (R14). The ban that waited for T13 is lifted: the engine gets lessons with a segment of a task with a rubric, and `api` begins their runs. "Left" and "abandoned" do not end such a segment, as they do not end any. The author's decision in the planning of T13; what this means for the engine is to В15 in table 3 of RUN.md. — R14; RUN.md, T13; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.7 and 3.8.

— *Amendment 2026-11-08:* the detailing of `engine` did not bring consolidation within a lesson and an early end of a lesson into the first slice (R107 spec; R05 engine), so "early" above is only a place for a new version of the engine: the contract has no such reason, and `api` does not call the engine at the end of a segment. At the engine's input are the profile's runs — the lesson, its skills, the end reason, empty for an open one, and the end time —, while the rule does not read the places of ended segments, and `api` does not pass them to the engine (R09 engine; concepts 12 spec): a call holds as much data as the rule reads (privacy 1 spec). The places of segments stay in the answers to `app` and in the refusals "segments not ended" and "segment unavailable". The skill chosen on the skill map holds at the end of a run too: `api` calls the engine with it, whatever code the choice that gave the lesson came with (R107 spec; the 2026-11-08 amendment to R06). — R24; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.7 and 3.9.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10* (T33; R43): one open run per profile and lesson is held by the unique partial index `lesson_runs (profile_id, lesson_id) WHERE ended_at IS NULL`, and one choice opens one run — `choice_id` is unique. An abandoned run — 30 days without actions — is ended by the step `abandoned_runs` of the job `api sweep`, the same way as `abandoned_attempts` closes attempts (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R10).

— *Amendment 2026-11-10* (T38; R48): the run — in code. The answer to the end of a segment names the place of the next segment of any kind — an explanation, an example or a task —, and after the last segment "no more segments": `app` keeps the places among the explanations itself. The answer by which a request ended a run does not yet carry the engine choice: the code of `engine` gives it (T40), and until then `app` asks for "something else". An action on a run is a request about it and an action on its attempt, and a run's last action is the later of its own and the attempt's; reading a segment's attempt does not move the run. A segment's attempt closed "unchecked" and its run are ended by one transaction — a database trigger, by whatever path the attempt was closed. A segment of a task that the code will not begin — with a rubric, on a workbook or with handwriting without admission to AI — is not checked by the lesson when it opens: the attempt of such a segment is "not admitted", and the run stays open (the author's decision; Д26 in table 4 of RUN.md).

— *Amendment 2026-11-10* (T40; R50): the choice at the end of a run — in code. When the request itself ended a run — "lesson passed" or a check that found the end —, `api` calls the engine with the skill that the last choice holds, and the choice goes into `RunEnd`: of the answer "lesson passed" or of the data of the refusal "run ended". A repeat of "lesson passed" gives the same choice, and a run that ended before the request carries no choice: `app` asks for "something else". Lessons whose task segment the code will not begin do not go to the engine (Д26).

## Task with a rubric

**R14. The attempt of a task with a rubric is open for rewriting and ends by the learner's "Done" — a new close reason "done", which also ends the lesson's segment. A submission with a rubric ends with feedback, "unreadable" or "unchecked", not with a verdict. Feedback that did not pass the feedback rule or the leak check is replaced by the phrase of `app` "no feedback this time", without repeating the call.**
- **What was decided** ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.8; sources — section 4):
  - the path is two calls of `tutor` in one request: reading the essay by one reader, then assessing the read text by the criteria of the rubric, without the picture (R75 spec; the second 2026-11-01 amendment to R68 spec). Before reading, `api` takes a reserve for reading together with the assessment (R12);
  - a submission with a rubric ends with one of three, and this is not a verdict — an item with a rubric has none (concepts 9.1 spec):
    - feedback — the assessment returned at least one criterion with an assessment. A criterion's mark is "met", "not met" or "not assessed", in the words of R75 and R71 spec, and not "not yet": that is the name of a value of a verdict. Not assessed is a criterion that is absent from the model's answer or that is named twice, and `api` does not repeat the call: this is the answer to the question that `tutor` left to `api` (architecture 3.5 tutor);
    - "unreadable" — reading said that the sheet as a whole cannot be read. The assessment is not called, and its reserve is returned (R12), and the learner sees a request to rewrite the sheet — the same text of `app` as for the verdict "unreadable";
    - "unchecked" — an AI failure, an exhausted quota, the admission refusal "not admitted", a provider's refusal by a limit, or an assessment that returned no criterion with an assessment. The learner sees a neutral phrase (R04 spec), and for the quota also the time of the next day (R11). The attempt waits, as R11 decided for any task.

    "Unreadable" and "unchecked" mean here the same as the verdicts of the same name — it is not the learner's error —, but they stand at a submission, not at an answer slot and an item. The three ends of a submission have no common name: "result" in concepts 15 spec already means both feedback and the end of an attempt;
  - feedback passes the leak check — for a rubric it searches only for the task's leak phrases ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 20) — and the feedback rule, by the code of `tutor` (R70 spec). If it passed, it goes to the device. If it did not, the learner sees the phrase of `app` "no feedback this time", and the call is not repeated. This is not the neutral phrase of R04 spec: that one says there is no check, and here there was an assessment, and only the words did not pass. The marks are recorded then too: the assessment was made, and only the words did not pass;
  - what goes to the device is the read text, if there was reading, and the words of the feedback or a note that there are none. The justifications, the marks and the criteria do not go: the learner sees feedback, not an assessment (concepts 9.1 spec), and the criteria are closed until the task is solved ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 15). Who sees the marks, R15 decides;
  - the attempt is open for rewriting: the learner corrects the same sheet and sends it again, and each submission is a new reserve and two calls again, and the quota holds the spending (R11). The assessment gets neither the earlier feedback nor the earlier text: they are not on the server (R15). A new submission after a lost answer is a new submission like any other. But a repeat of the request with the same idempotency key does not call AI again: `api` answers with what it recorded — how the submission ended —, without what was read and the words of the feedback, which it does not store (R15), so a network failure does not spend the quota twice. Idempotency keys — T07;
  - closing. "Done" is a learning action, `can_learn` (R04). `api` accepts it after at least one submission of the attempt, however that ended, and before that refuses it with the cause "no submission". The attempt is closed with the reason "done" in one transaction, without evidence (О-17; R06) — the 2026-11-02 amendment to R10. "Done" after "unreadable" or "unchecked" is the way out for a learner whose essay cannot be read and for one whose quota has run out. "Left", "abandoned", "unchecked" on a withdrawn version or outside the window, and "not suitable" close an attempt with a rubric like any other (R07, R10, R13), and none of them gives evidence;
  - two devices. `api` refuses a submission to a closed attempt before calling AI. If the attempt was closed while its submission was going, how the submission ended is not recorded: the answer carries the feedback from the request's memory and says that the attempt is closed, and the quota is counted by R12;
  - the lesson. The segment of a task with a rubric ends when its attempt is closed "done". "Left" and "abandoned" do not end it, as for any segment (R13). The ban of R13 is lifted: the engine gets lessons with a segment of a task with a rubric, and `api` begins their runs — the 2026-11-02 amendment to R13. After "done" outside a lesson the engine chooses the next one, as after any attempt (architecture 3.5 spec);
  - there is no ladder, no showing the solution and no "explain differently" (R09). "Hint" gives what R10 gives before the first wrong answer: the "givens and unknown" and the task's technique, if it has them (R57 spec). If it has neither, the refusal "nothing to hint": the techniques are closed, and `app` does not know this in advance. How the refusal looks in the protocol, T07 decides;
  - the quota: `api` begins a new task with a rubric only if what is left is enough for a typical task with a rubric — two readings with an assessment, ≈ $0.06 —, and this is a configuration value of its own (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R11). An own key does not spend the quota (R11);
  - logs — without IDs and without content (R09 spec): the event code "feedback did not pass the check" with the part of the rule that the feedback broke — sentences, words, quote, letters (architecture 3.5 tutor) — or with a leak. By it one sees how often the phrase "no feedback" replaces feedback;
  - what went «в реестр spec» is В17, В18 and В19 in table 3 of RUN.md.

— *Why:* the author's decision of 2026-11-02 by the recommendations that the planning of T13 proposed, and by the findings of its independent review.
- **"Done", not the first feedback.** Feedback says what worked and what to work on, by the criteria (architecture 3.5 tutor), and rewriting is part of writing: in the prototype the essay goes "Plan, Write, Improve" (`spec/draft/README.md`), and the example graph of the module of essays has the skill `improving-an-essay` (`spec/format/examples/skill-graph/valid/short-essays.yaml`). If the first feedback closed the attempt, in a lesson it would be impossible to rewrite after it: a segment's attempt begins only while the segment has not ended (R13). And feedback lost in the network or that did not pass the rule could not be returned: the server does not store its words (R15). "Done" — after any submission, so that a learner whose essay cannot be read, or whose quota has run out, is not stuck on the segment: a task with a rubric has no showing the solution, by which the segment of a task with a key ends (R09).
- **The phrase "no feedback".** The format gives a rubric no fallback text: fallback texts belong to the rungs of the ladder, after a wrong value ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 19), and a template of the platform would have to be translated into the language of each module — R57 spec moved away from that, giving the templates of rungs to the module. A repeat is one more call of the assessment beyond the reserve (R12) and one more wait, while an essay at Opus 5.5 · `high` takes ≈ 12 s as it is. Its feedback broke the rule of R70 spec in 2 essays of 45 (T92; architecture 3.4 tutor), so the learner will see the phrase "no feedback" rarely, and the attempt is open, and they can send the essay again themselves.
- **A criterion without an assessment — without a repeat**, at the same price of a repeat. The mark "not assessed" is more honest than a mark made at random (R04 spec).
- **"Unreadable" — only the sheet as a whole.** A single illegible word must not drive the whole essay to rewriting — the same argument by which a sheet under a rubric is read by one reader (the second 2026-11-01 amendment to R68 spec). How reading gives "unreadable", `tutor` has not yet measured (architecture 3.5 tutor) — В17 is to it.
- **"Done", not "assessed".** The reason names the learner's action, not the assessment, which the learner must not see (concepts 9.1 spec).

— *Rejected:*
- the first feedback closes the attempt, and rewriting is a new attempt of the same task: in a lesson it is impossible to rewrite after feedback, feedback lost or that did not pass the rule cannot be returned, and for a template a new attempt is a new seed and perhaps another topic (R60 spec);
- "Done" only after feedback or two "unreadable" in a row: the rule is more complex, and with an exhausted quota the segment would wait until tomorrow;
- "Done" before the first submission: the learner would skip the essay without sending a single sheet. After a submission they can end the attempt even with an empty sheet that gave "unreadable" — but an adult sees this in the attempt's submissions (R15), and one whose essay cannot be read has no other way out;
- one reason for any attempt with feedback, however it was closed — by "Done", by leaving or by the term: the reason would have two meanings. A learner who left sends the essay again on return — it is on the device (R04 app) —, and for a template writes a new one: a new attempt is a new seed and perhaps another topic (R60 spec), as a task with a key is solved again on return (R13);
- one repeat of the assessment when feedback did not pass the rule: the arguments are above;
- a fallback text of the module — a blank of feedback or an advice at a criterion: format v0 has none, and this is a change of the format of `spec`;
- showing the criteria with marks instead of feedback: the learner would see an assessment, not feedback (concepts 9.1 spec), and the criteria are closed;
- "unreadable" as an item's verdict: an item with a rubric has no verdict (concepts 9.1 spec).

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-02 amendments to R10 — the reason "done" —, to R11 — the typical task with a rubric — and to R13 — the segment ends "done", the ban is lifted. What R09 (the path without the ladder), R11 (an essay with an exhausted quota) and R13 (the end of a segment) left to T13 is decided here. The table of actions of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.1, gets "Done". T07 carries the answer to a submission with a rubric, "Done" and the refusals "no submission", "attempt closed" and "nothing to hint". «В реестр spec» — В17, В18 and В19 and additions to В10, В11, В15 and В16 in table 3 of RUN.md. — RUN.md, T07, T13; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2.10, 3.1, 3.5, 3.6, 3.7 and 3.8; R10, R11, R13.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* there is no refusal "nothing to hint". "Hint" on a task with a rubric gets the same as a request before the first wrong answer: the IDs of the task's techniques, empty if there are none, and `app` shows the "givens and unknown" from the task's open view (R82 spec; the 2026-11-02 amendment to R10). The empty list is the answer that there is nothing to hint. — R16; RUN.md, T07; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.8.

**R15. Of an attempt of a task with a rubric the server stores how each submission ended, and for a submission with feedback, the mark of each criterion and the source of the words. It stores neither the essay's text, nor the justifications, nor the words of the feedback: they live in the request's memory and on the device. This is `api`'s recommendation to П-19. The marks are seen by an adult in progress when the attempt is closed, and the learner sees the feedback.**
- **What was decided** ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.8; sources — section 4):
  - an attempt with a rubric is the same row as any attempt: the package digest, the module and its version, the task, the close reason and the times, and for a lesson's segment, the run ID and the place (R07, R10, R13). It has no fields of the ladder: a rubric has no wrong answers, rungs or showing the solution (R09);
  - each submission has its time and how it ended: with feedback, "unreadable" or "unchecked" (R14). Feedback has the mark of each criterion by its number in the rubric — "met", "not met" or "not assessed" —, and the source of the words: the model or the phrase "no feedback", as the source of the words of hints in R05. A criterion has no ID, its number in order names it ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 15; architecture 3.5 tutor), and the number is read by the attempt's package digest;
  - not stored — neither in the database, nor in the repeat queue, nor in the cache of answers or of idempotency, nor in the logs — are the crop of the sheet (R09 spec), the read text of the essay, the justifications and the words of the feedback. They live in the request's memory, and what was read and the feedback also on the device, with the essay (R04 app). An essay has no observation row, and the words of the feedback, unlike the words of hints (R05), do not live even until the end of the attempt — the 2026-11-02 amendment to R05;
  - the term is while the profile lives (П-9). Everything listed goes into the export: a criterion by the task ID, the module version and the number, and the export is read by the package of an official module, which is open (repositories 4 spec). It is deleted with the profile (T08);
  - who reads the marks. The learner — no: they see feedback, not an assessment (concepts 9.1 spec). An adult — in progress, when the attempt is closed: for one closed "done", the essay is finished, and for one closed otherwise, the rewriting was abandoned (product 3 spec). «В реестр spec» — a request to count a task with a rubric as solved when its attempt is closed "done", and then to give its criteria and its solution, if there is one (В19). Until `spec` has accepted this, `api` gives neither the criteria nor the solution of a task with a rubric: they are closed ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), sections 6, 14 and 15), and the adult sees the marks by the numbers of the criteria. What stands behind a number, they will read in the feedback that `app` keeps with the essay on the device (R04 app): the feedback says what to work on, by the criteria (architecture 3.5 tutor). concepts 11 spec derives "what to develop" from the learner model, and rubric feedback does not go into it (О-17): the marks in progress are a reservation to concepts 11 spec, also В19;
  - `api` does not store the essay's text: only the account owner's choice can store it as an artifact (П-8), and П-8 is open at `spec` v0.6.2. `spec` v0.6.5 — a tag newer than the submodule — has already supplemented П-8: storing essays by the owner's choice comes with the essay module, before the first child. So it will come as an amendment to this entry with `api`'s move to that tag — Д12 in table 4 of RUN.md;
  - check against privacy spec (privacy 1, 4, 5.1, 7, 9 spec):
    - the marks are pseudonymous data, an opinion of AI on the learner's writing (privacy 1 spec). They hold no content of the work;
    - the promise of privacy 7 spec "What the learner writes by hand stays on the device" holds: neither the text nor the crop is on the server. Feedback may quote the essay (П-19), so it is not stored either;
    - what goes to AI is what privacy 5.1 spec names: for reading, the crop of the sheet; for the assessment, the read text without the picture, and the criteria of the rubric. Nothing of what is stored goes: the assessment does not get the earlier feedback;
    - into the logs — neither the text, nor the feedback, nor the justifications, nor marks with IDs (R09 spec);
    - П-20: since the server does not store the text, nobody on the platform will see a child's disclosure, in an essay, of harm to themselves or from others. What the platform should do with such a disclosure, П-20 already asks of the lawyer and of epic E4 in RUN.md of `spec`. The reminder not to write names is `app`'s (П-20);
  - the repeat of the engine choice: the close reason and time of an attempt, once recorded, do not change, and if the engine gets the closed attempts of tasks with a rubric (В18), the repeat restores them by the time of the choice — the 2026-11-02 amendment to R06;
  - `api`'s recommendation to П-19 is В17 in table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec».

— *Why:* the author's decision of 2026-11-02 by the recommendation of П-19, which the planning of T13 proposed too. Progress needs the marks — what to develop —, and does not need the text and the words (П-19). There is no need to repeat feedback from the observation: R03 spec repeats a verdict, and feedback has none (П-19), — so what was read, which R05 keeps with the profile for the sake of repeating a verdict, an essay does not need. Otherwise the server would keep an essay without the owner's choice, against П-8 and privacy 7 spec. The marks go to an adult after "done", because the learner sees feedback, not an assessment, and to an adult they say what to work on.

— *Rejected:*
- everything, as for any attempt — the read text, the words of the feedback and the assessment: the server would keep an essay without the owner's choice (П-8; privacy 7 spec);
- only the fact of the attempt: progress would have nothing to show (П-19);
- the words of the feedback until the end of the attempt, as the words of hints (R05): nothing reads them, and the quotes of the essay in them are its text;
- the marks only in the export: they would not serve progress, for whose sake they are stored;
- the marks to the learner too: a list of marks is closer to an assessment than to feedback (concepts 9.1 spec).

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-02 amendments to R05 — an essay has neither what was read nor the words of the feedback —, and to R06 — the source of the repeat. The table of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2, has the rows "attempt with a rubric" and "artifacts, essay text", and the row "observation" says that an essay has none. T08 deletes and exports the marks, and T07 keeps the text and the feedback out of the caches. «В реестр spec» — В17 and В19 and additions to В3 and В4 in table 3 of RUN.md, and storing essays by the owner's choice — Д12 in table 4. — RUN.md, T07, T08, T13; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2.8, 2.10, 3.2 and 3.8; R05, R06.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* П-8 was supplemented by В9 of `app`: storing essays on the server by the account owner's choice comes with the essay module, before the first child — otherwise the only copy of a learner's work depends on the browser's rules —, and until then the page «Это устройство» and the sign-out warn of the loss (privacy 9 spec). With the move to this tag (R16) this is a requirement on `api`: before the first child the server stores an essay if the account owner chose so, with deletion in one step (П-8). What of an essay is stored — the text, what was read, the words of the feedback —, where, and how it goes into the export and the deletion, a task of its own will decide — Д12 in table 4 of RUN.md. Until then — as in this entry: there is no essay text on the server, and the first app does not need it: it holds only the author's essays (R76 spec). — R16; RUN.md, Д12; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.8.

— *Amendment 2026-11-07:* `spec` accepted `api`'s recommendation: a task with a rubric is solved when its attempt is closed "done", and then its criteria and its solution, if the task has one, are open (R87 spec; the second 2026-11-02 amendment to R05 spec). "Until `spec` has accepted this, `api` gives neither the criteria nor the solution" no longer holds: "Done" answers with the solved task — the criteria and the solution (`SolvedTask`, R97 spec) —, while "left", "abandoned", "unchecked" and "not suitable" do not open them. The list of criteria with marks does not go to the learner. The words of the marks are "met", "not met" and "not assessed" (R87 spec). — R23; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.8.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14* (T75; R70): the storage of essays at the owner's choice is built. An essay is kept as a stored work — its strokes and its picture, as the device holds them, of the kind "essay" by the task's rubric — while whoever consents for the learner keeps the storage of the profile's works on (R113 spec). What was read and the words of the feedback stay off the server, as this entry has it, and Д12 is closed. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2 and 3.8.

## The `spec` tag: v0.6.6

**R16. `api` stands on `spec` v0.6.6. The tag records `spec`'s answers to rows В1–В14 of table 3 of RUN.md, the rules of the contracts `app` ↔ `api` with the transport Connect, the own key in the contract, showing the solution and a hint at the learner's request, and epic E3, which writes the contract `app` ↔ `api`, has become current and waits for T07. There are no new points of the card. Additions to recorded rows that `spec` did not see went as new rows.**
- **What the tag brings `api`** (`git -C spec diff v0.6.2 v0.6.6`, read 2026-11-02; table 5 and the parts for `api` in rows Д61–Д80 of table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`; rows 1.8, 6.1, 6.2 and 6.5 of `COORDINATION.md` of `spec`):
  - the answers to В1–В14:
    - В1–В3 — additions to П-17, П-11, П-9 and П-6, the rows "Account" and "Logs" of privacy 4 spec with a reference to sections 3.1 and 3.2 in [architecture.md](architecture.md), and the OpenFGA store in architecture 5 spec (Д62);
    - В4 — R80 spec: the learner model is in `api`'s database, the log of evidence is the source of truth, the state is a cache; П-7 and Т-22 are closed. В5 — additions to П-9 and П-11 and the row "Hosting and infrastructure" of privacy 2 spec (Д67);
    - В6 — the second 2026-11-02 amendment to R63 spec: the name of a package's file is derived from the index entry — the archive `sha256-<hex>.tar.gz` (architecture 3.10 spec). The fix of a withdrawn newest major is a version above it by semver, not necessarily of the same major, as `api` asked: a change of the key is itself major (concepts 3 spec). `api`'s argument — otherwise the previous major would become current, and the skills of the withdrawn one would have nothing to be carried over by — is accepted: the fix comes out in the same release that withdraws. В7 — an addition to Т-19: `api` is rolled back only by rolling forward. В8 — the 2026-11-02 amendment to R80 spec, with a clarification about the anchor skill; В9 — the 2026-11-02 amendment to R49 spec (Д70);
    - В10 and В11 — R82 spec: О-7 and О-21 are closed by `api`'s recommendations, with the clarifications of the review (Д73);
    - В12–В14 — additions to О-14 and П-9, a new question П-23 about the clock offset, concepts 12, 14 and 15 spec, privacy 4 spec and the cards architecture 3.5, 3.6 and 3.8 spec (Д76);
  - the additions to В3, В4, В10, В11 and В14 that T03, T12 and T13 made, `spec` did not see: it recorded these rows by commits earlier than these tasks, and `just inbox` matches rows by number (R81 spec). The addition to В10 — the function of the feedback rule from `tutor` — the card architecture 3.5 spec already carries, the one to В14 — the open В15, and those to В3 about essays and to В11 about "done" — the open В17. The rest went as new rows: В20 — the format window in the cache mark and the time of the choice, В21 — lesson runs in privacy 4 spec, В22 — the reason "not suitable";
  - R83 spec — the rules of contracts: proto3 files in `contracts/`, packages `novatrail.<part>.v1`, buf with the categories `STANDARD`, `COMMENTS` and `UNARY_RPC` and compatibility `FILE` against the previous release; the name of a field in JSON is the proto name, snake_case; the contract's version is the package suffix and the tag of `spec`, and `api` keeps the current and the previous major package. `spec` generates the code: in Go, the nested module `github.com/altnovatrail/spec/contracts` by the tag `contracts/vX.Y.Z`, in TypeScript, `@altnovatrail/contracts`. `app` and `api` speak Connect, and whether JSON or binary protobuf is on the wire, T07 decides. With it come the 2026-11-02 amendments to R11, R21, R44, R64, R72 and R73 spec. For T07 this decides the versions of the contract (Д20 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`) and the names of fields in JSON, and how the open view is laid out, T64 of epic E3 decides (Д21);
  - R84 spec — the own key: on the device, by the choice of the key's owner, on the server, only in the request's memory; the key and the provider come with each request of the owner's profile that calls AI, and with "explain differently" too; on connecting, `api` checks the key through `tutor`. Т-9 and П-17 are closed. Hence the 2026-11-02 amendment to R04, and the requests of the key are T07 (Д78);
  - R82 spec — hence the 2026-11-02 amendments to R10 and R14. The 2026-11-02 amendment to R05 spec: the key and the task's solution go to the device only when the attempt is closed by a correct answer or by showing the solution. The 2026-11-02 amendment to R69 spec: "step done" does not read the work, — and the estimate of the spending "by steps" in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.6, ≈ $1.10 by reading at each step, is wrong; `spec` rewrote it in О-14, and the amendment to it is В23;
  - R80 spec and its amendment — hence the 2026-11-02 amendments to R06 — the choice stores whether the pool was limited to tasks without AI —, and to R08 — the anchor skill by the vocabulary of some format version in `api`'s window;

The amendment to R03 — `app` takes media files by `fetch`, not by `<img>` — is for T07 (Д79), and with it the 2026-11-02 amendment to R36 spec: the element gets a picture as a `Blob`;
  - epic E3 is current: T62–T64 write the contract `app` ↔ `api` and wait for T07, and T63 also for T08. Its table 1 has «Что `app` нужно от контракта в E3», «Свой ключ в контракте `app` ↔ `api`», «origin `api` и версии» and erasing on the device. Д71, Д74, Д78 and Д79 are to T07, Д68 to T08;

  - repositories 4 spec — `api` takes a tag of `tutor` with a new model only after the merge of a pull request of `modules` that ran the modules' eval sets on it. Т-35 is closed by the 2026-11-02 amendment to R63 spec, and Т-25 is supplemented: together with the voice, decide whether a channel is needed if voice input goes as a stream. The card architecture 3.5 spec names the learner's default mode — a field of its own —, and voicing — in the presentation style (concepts 10 spec);
  - R78, R79 and R81 spec, the amendments to R25, R39, R57, R58, R62, R74 and R77 spec — about other components, tools and the wording of the format text. They do not change `api`'s documents, except rule 2 of RUN.md by R81 spec;
  - the template: the tag in CLAUDE.md, README.md and RUN.md and rule 2 of RUN.md — a row «В реестр spec» opens with this mark, and `spec`'s session finds it itself (R81 spec); the devcontainer — buf 1.75.0 in the stage `toolchain` (R83 spec) and the comments of the Dockerfile (Д75 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`).
- **What changed here:**
  - `spec/` — at v0.6.6, commit 5d8765e. The tag in CLAUDE.md, README.md and RUN.md is v0.6.6; in CLAUDE.md — Your role and The component — and in README.md, Status, the learner model is no longer an open question (R80 spec), and The component names the module of contracts (R83 spec);

  - RUN.md — table 3: rows В1–В14 closed, new В20–В24; table 4: Д4, Д8, Д11, Д12 and the new Д13; T07, T08 and T09 — what the tag decided for them.

— *Why now:* the author's decision of 2026-11-02 in the planning of T07. After v0.6.2 `spec` decided the rules of contracts, the transport and the own key in the contract, and T07 must refer to them, and the documents check matches citations against the submodule's tag (R32 spec). Д62, Д67, Д70, Д73 and Д76 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec` wait for `api` to close В1–В14 by its move. v0.6.6 is the latest release as of 2026-11-02 and immutable: `gh release verify v0.6.6 -R altnovatrail/spec` confirmed it by its attestation, the tag is on commit 5d8765e.

— *Rejected:*

- T14 and T07 in a row in one session: two tasks without the author's review between them (rule 1 of RUN.md);
- closing В3, В4 and В11 whole: the additions of T12 and T13, which `spec` did not see, would vanish from the register;
