## The own key in every choice

**R65. Every choice of the engine goes by the route to AI that the request names, as `BeginAttempt` by the same route would begin a task: `ChooseNext`, `next` of `SubmitWork` and `ShowSolution`, and the end of a lesson run that `OpenLesson`, `GetLessonRun`, `CompleteLesson` or the beginning of a segment gives. On the own key the pool, the lessons and the runs that wait are judged by the admission of its provider, with no quota and no flag, and "nothing to give" carries "not admitted" alone. A profile not of the category `adult` that names a provider is refused `CAUSE_KEY_NOT_FOR_PROFILE`, and `SubmitWork` with `own_key_provider` beside `work` or `own_key`, `invalid_argument`. The route is not written to the choice's record.** Task T68 of epic E9, 2026-11-13; one task, not two, and no route in the record were decided by the author in planning, by the executor's recommendation.
- **What was decided** (the contract `contracts/v0.16.3` and later, T143 spec; the 2026-11-12 amendments to R84 and R99 spec; Д224 spec):
  - **the route** is the provider of the own key without the key, `own_key_provider`, at `ChooseNext`, `OpenLesson`, `GetLessonRun`, `CompleteLesson`, `ShowSolution` and `Done`, and `BeginAttempt` names it by its own field. At `SubmitWork` it is the provider of `own_key`, or `own_key_provider` when the submission sends neither `work` nor `own_key` — a task a tap checks. Empty is platform AI;
  - **the choice** — `choose` in `internal/learning/choose.go` asks `Gate.Handwriting(category, provider)`, and `closedTo` — the quota and the flag — only on platform AI (R59). `candidates`, `begins`, `waits` and `history` take the provider, so that the pool, the lessons and an open run that waits (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R59) go by `admitted(task, category, provider)`, the same rule as the beginning (R50). An admitted own key leaves no task out for AI, so "nothing to give" carries an outcome there only when its provider is not admitted — `closedCause` gives "not admitted";
  - **the requests that give a choice:** `nextChoice`, `nextAfter`, `endRun` and the checks of a run — `checkRun`, `checkRunGiving`, `holdRun`, `openRun` and `segmentBegun` — carry the provider of the request; the end of a run that `BeginAttempt` of a segment finds gives a choice by that `BeginAttempt`'s provider. A repeat gives the choice from the record (R50): under an idempotency key it names the same route, as below, and a repeat of "lesson passed", which goes by the state of the run, gets the recorded choice whatever route it names;
  - **the refusals:** `ownKeyFits` at each of these requests, before anything is read or written; `Done` gives no `next` yet (R14), and checks the provider all the same; at `SubmitWork`, `own_key_provider` beside `work` or `own_key` is `invalid_argument`, after the checks of the own key and of the provider, so that a child's request gets "key not for this profile" as everywhere;
  - **the record** stays as it is: the admission of handwriting depends on the category and the provider, not on the task, so the pool is either whole or of the tasks a tap checks, and `pool_limited` with `age_category` is what a replay needs. On the own key "nothing to give" has one cause, `not_admitted`, which `nothing_cause` of the migration `0016` keeps;
  - **the idempotency key:** `own_key_provider` is part of a request's fingerprint (`internal/idem` leaves out `idempotency_key` and `own_key` alone), so a repeat that names another provider is `CAUSE_KEY_ALREADY_USED`, as at `BeginAttempt`.
- **Checked:** the tests — the pool by the route, by the hook `Candidates` too: platform AI closed by the deployment and the own key of OpenAI leave the tasks a tap checks, the own key of Anthropic at the stage "author" gives the handwriting; the quota spent and the flag set: the owner's run whose next segment is of handwriting comes next by the own key and its segment begins by it, while on platform AI it waits; `next` of a tap's submission and of the solution shown, the end of "lesson done", and the end "abandoned" that `OpenLesson`, `GetLessonRun` and the beginning of a segment find, come from a pool not limited by the own key, and the same end without a provider from a limited one; a provider not admitted gives "not admitted" alone; a child's provider is refused at six requests, and `SubmitWork` with a provider beside the work or the key. A run on the dev projects by R146 spec, with no paid call — the server's key one Anthropic refuses, no line `ai_usage` — and the counter of the dev account's family day filled past its caps and put back after: on platform AI 8 choices gave tasks a tap checks, by the own key of Anthropic 8 gave the run whose segment of handwriting began by that key, by the own key of OpenAI 3 gave tasks a tap checks.

— *Why:*
- the contract requires it (R84 and R99 spec, Д224 spec): an owner with an own key whose platform quota is spent would get only tasks a tap checks, or "nothing to give" with the quota, though the beginning by the key takes the handwriting;
- one task: the route is one parameter through the same functions, and between two tasks `ChooseNext` and `next` would go by different routes — the 2026-11-12 amendment in T143 to R84 spec rejected `ChooseNext` alone with the rest as a debt;
- no route in the record: a replay needs nothing more, and the record keeps no data of which provider the owner uses, which the export of `spec` does not carry either.

— *Rejected:*
- two tasks — `ChooseNext` with its pool, then `next` with the end of a run;
- a column of the provider or of the route in `engine_choices`, migration `0017`: new data of the owner in the database and the export with nothing that reads it.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-13 amendments to R50, R59 and R62; Д224 spec answered for `api`; what `app` sends is T46 `app` (row 6.14 of COORDINATION.md spec). — RUN.md, T68; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.6, 3.7 and 3.9.

## The stub of tutor in the dev mode

**R66. In the dev mode `api` calls a stub of `tutor` instead of `tutor`'s reading of a sheet and words: `API_TUTOR_STUB=1`, refused on a deployment, as R32 refuses the emulator, and beside a key of platform AI. The stub reads a sheet by the shape of its crop — wider than tall the right value, taller than wide a wrong one, square illegible —, words questions of its own, and spends what `tutor` expects the call to cost; admission and the prices stay `tutor`'s. `just dev` runs on the stub, `just dev-ai` on the models.** Task T71 of epic E9, 2026-11-13; the reading by the shape, the expected price and `just dev` on the stub were decided by the author in planning, by the executor's recommendation. The first half of Д33; the answer of `api` to R146 spec and Д226 spec.
- **What was decided:**
  - **the switch** is `API_TUTOR_STUB=1` in `config.Admission`, so `api check` refuses it before the migrations too. The dev mode is off a deployment: neither `K_SERVICE` nor `CLOUD_RUN_JOB` is set, the rule of the stage `author` (R45) and of the key of the service account (R32). With the switch, a variable of a deployment, a key of Anthropic — `API_ANTHROPIC_KEY` or its `_FILE` — or a value other than `1` is a refusal of the configuration that names the variables. The lines `startup` and `admission` carry `tutor_stub`;
  - **admission** — `admission.Load` gives `tutor` the stub's adapter, `admission.StubAdapters`, and a key `stub` that stands for the platform's: the adapter makes the client of Claude's catalog with no key, whose names and prices `tutor` reads for the choice and `Tutor.Price`, and refuses every call and every check of a key. So `ChooseCall`, the pool, the start of a task and the reserve of the quota go on the real table, facts and stage, and no SDK client with a key exists in the process;
  - **the stub** is the package `internal/tutorstub`, behind the ports of `internal/learning`: `sheetReader` and `wordsTeller`, by the option `learning.WithTutor`, which `serve` gives with the switch. Its admission of the words is the gate's;
  - **the reading.** The crop's size, by `png.DecodeConfig`, picks the value of the one slot bound anywhere, which `module-kit`'s verdict finds on the instance (`verdict.OfSlot`), out of the key, the options of a choice written by hand and the whole numbers 0–999:
    - wider than tall — the first value it holds right;
    - taller than wide — the first trap value it holds not right, else the first such value of the rest;
    - square, or no value found — `UNREADABLE_ILLEGIBLE`.

    Both readers read the same: a value with the crop box of the whole crop, `0 0 1000 1000` in thousandths, and a reason with no box, as the rules of an observation have it. A crop that is no PNG, a task with no slot bound anywhere, or a request cut short fails as `tutor`'s reading does;
  - **the port of reading** carries the instance: `sheetReader.ReadSheet(ctx, reading, task)`. The gate's adapter `gateReader` drops it, so `tutor` still sees the open view alone (R67 spec); only the stub reads the key;
  - **the words** — three questions each in English and Russian, without a number, which pass the leak check and the form check; the first that `Asked` does not hold, so "explain differently" gets other words. Another language gets English, which the form check turns down into the fallback text;
  - **the spending** — the expected price of the kind of call on the route, `Tutor.Price`, which is the reserve (R58), shared by the readers in whole micro-dollars; a kind of call with no expected price spends nothing priced, and the reserve stays spent, as R12 has it. So the settlement spends the reserve to the micro-dollar, and the quota runs out after as many calls as on the models. The usage names the provider `anthropic` and the model `stub`. The flag "platform limit" the stub never sets: it gives no "limit reached";
  - **the runs** — `just dev` (`scripts/dev.sh run`) unsets the dev file's key of platform AI and sets the switch; `just dev-ai` (`run-ai`) keeps the key and unsets the switch, for a task that checks the words of a model under the cap its text names (rule 4 of RUN.md). `just dev-check` stays on the key Anthropic refuses (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R39) and unsets the switch.
- **Checked:** the tests — the configuration and its refusals; `serve` refused on a deployment and started on the stub; the gate of the stub admits and prices with no key, and its models answer nothing; the stub reads a number with a trap and without, a predicate and a choice by hand by the three shapes, each reading one that `module-kit`'s `observation.Join` takes, spends the reserve exactly, fails what `tutor` fails and a request cut short, and rotates its words; on the database, the path — a square crop illegible, a tall crop "not yet", the rung's words of the stub, other words for "explain differently", a wide crop "correct" and solved, the counters spending the expected prices with nothing in reserve, the log with the model `stub`; `dev.sh` puts the run on the stub or on the models.

— *Why:*
- R146 spec has the executor of `app` walk the path with no paid call, and the stub has to give both "correct" and a wrong answer, or the hint ladder cannot be walked;
- behind the ports of `learning`, not as an adapter of `tutor`: an adapter sees the open view and the crop, never the key (R67 spec), and could read no right value, while `tutor` would still admit, choose and price with it;
- the shape of the crop is what a scenario of Playwright can draw — a horizontal or a vertical stroke — without recognizing handwriting;
- the expected price makes the quota run with `app` that R146 spec asks for possible on the stub;
- `just dev` on the stub: a session cannot spend money by mistake, and a real call is the exception a task names.

— *Rejected:*
- the stub as `provider.Dial` alone: no right value, as above;
- the reading always right — no wrong answer, no ladder — or always illegible — neither;
- a stub that spends nothing — the quota could not be run out on it;
- a build tag that leaves the stub out of the release: the refusal at the start is the guarantee R32 gives the emulator, and lint and coverage would need a second build;
- `just dev` on the models with `just dev-stub` beside it, and `dev-check` on the stub: the check would no longer reach the adapter of Claude.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-13 amendments to R39, R46 and R54; Д33 narrowed to the tool of a custom token, T72; В64 «в реестр spec» — the shapes a scenario of `app` draws. — RUN.md, T71; README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.11.

— *Amendment 2026-11-13* (T73; R68): the stub spends the expected price of the kind of call of its request: the words with a crop `CallWords`, without one `CallHint`, which `tutor` v0.1.33 gives no expected price, so "explain differently" on the stub spends nothing priced and the quota keeps its reserve as spent (R12).

**R67. The local build of `app` signs in by a custom token of the test account `dev-app`, which `just dev-app` lays in the file `custom-token` of the Docker volume `novatrail-dev-token`, anew every half hour, while it keeps `api` up for `app` on the stub of `tutor`; `app` mounts the volume read-only. The recipe is over `scripts/dev.sh`, with no Go code, and the volume is the third difference of the devcontainer of `api` from the container of `spec`.** Task T72 of epic E9, 2026-11-13; the volume and the account of its own were decided by the author in planning, by the executor's recommendation. The second half of Д33; the answer of `api` to R146 spec and Д226 spec.
- **What was decided:**
  - **`just dev-app`** (`scripts/dev.sh app`) runs on the stub (R66): the build, `migrate` and the country `US`, the packages of the pinned release laid afresh into a folder of its own run, as `dev-check` lays them, `api reopen`, `serve` on port 8080 on those packages with its log in the terminal, then an ID token of `dev-app` and the account by an invite code with an owner's profile of the category `adult`, by the same steps as `dev-check`, so that a repeat makes nothing. Then it lays the custom token and lays it anew every 1800 s until the server stops or Ctrl-C. On the way out it stops the server, deletes the file and a temporary one half laid — a token past its hour is not left behind —, and checks the server's log as `dev-check` does, for the custom token too: a log that fails fails the run. A run that stops before it lays its first token — a second one beside a run that goes on, whose server finds port 8080 taken — leaves the file where it is. It prints the path of the file and the address `hostname -i`:8080, never the token;
  - **the account** — the UID `API_DEV_APP_UID`, by default `dev-app`, apart from `dev-check`'s `dev-author`: the runs of `app` spend the quota of their own profile, by the expected prices of the stub, and leave their attempts there;
  - **the file** — `/home/vscode/.novatrail-token/custom-token`, or the folder `API_DEV_TOKEN_DIR`: one line, a token with the claims of R39 and `uid` `dev-app`, an hour long, so the file always holds one with half an hour left. It is written to a temporary file of the same folder, mode `600`, and renamed over the old one, so `app` never reads half a token. A folder that is not there is refused with a word of Rebuild Container, not made: `app` would not see it;
  - **the volume** — `novatrail-dev-token` at `/home/vscode/.novatrail-token`, which the Dockerfile makes as `vscode` with the mode `700`, as it does `.novatrail-dev`. A volume can be mounted read-write in one container and read-only in another (sources 2.7). The container of `app` is `vscode` on the same base image, `mcr.microsoft.com/devcontainers/base:3.0.12-noble` (`.devcontainer/Dockerfile` of `app`, `main` d5ceae6), so the mode lets it read. Docker copies the folder of the image into a new volume, but whether its owner passes too, the page does not say (sources 2.7); in this container the volume `novatrail-api-dev` belongs to `vscode` with the mode `700`, as its folder in the image. So the image of `app` makes the folder as `vscode` too, whichever container makes the volume first;
  - **the functions** `custom_token` and `id_token` take the UID; `exchange` swaps a custom token on stdin for an ID token; the steps of the legal texts, of the account, of the migrations, of the packages, of `reopen` and of the start of the server are functions that `check` and `app` share;
  - **the release** has no tool: the image takes from the build only `/api` and the folder of the packages, and the binary has no new code.
- **Checked 2026-11-13** on the projects `novatrail-api-dev`, with the folder of the token in the scratchpad, before the volume was mounted: the first run made the account `dev-app` and its owner's profile; the token of the file, exchanged by `exchange`, gave an ID token of `dev-app` by the provider `custom`, and `GetAccount` answered 200 with the owner's profile `adult` both on `127.0.0.1` and on the address of the network `novatrail-api`; Ctrl-C stopped the server and deleted the file, the log was clean; the repeat made nothing, and a server killed from outside ended the run at once; a second run beside the first stopped on the taken port and left the first one's token, which still signed in. After the author's Rebuild Container the same day, the volume was at `/home/vscode/.novatrail-token`, `vscode` with the mode `700`, and a run with no `API_DEV_TOKEN_DIR` laid the token there, signed in by it on both addresses and left the volume empty after Ctrl-C. `dev-check` passed after the change. The tests: the token of the UID asked for, its file `600` taking the old one's place with nothing left, the refusal of a folder that is not there and of no key. No call of AI, $0.

— *Why:*
- the executor of `app` cannot run a recipe in the container of `api`, and a custom token lasts an hour at most (sources 2.6): a fresh one has to lie where `app` takes it alone;
- a file in a volume is how the devcontainers of NovaTrail share their sign-ins already, `novatrail-claude` and `novatrail-gh`, and needs no port and no new language;
- the server and the token in one recipe: `app` needs both, and the author starts one thing;
- an account of its own keeps the quota of `dev-check` from running out by the runs of `app`.

— *Rejected:*
- a script of Node on the network `novatrail-api` that issues a token per request: no devcontainer changes, but a new language in `scripts/` and a port that signs in any container of the network;
- a one-off `just dev-token`: the author would lay the token before each run, against R146 spec;
- the volume `novatrail-api-dev` read-only in `app`: it holds the key of the service account;
- `dev-author`: the runs of `app` would spend its daily quota, and the step of the quota of `dev-check` would hit the cap; a UID with no account and an invite code each run: new code for each run, and the account deleted after;
- a Go program or a route of the binary in the dev mode: R39 rejected a second adapter of the sign-in service, and the task keeps the tool out of the release.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-13 amendments to R30 and R39; Д33 closed; В65 «в реестр spec» — what `app` takes. The author's step, Rebuild Container of `api`, is done. — RUN.md, T72; README.md; `.claude/rules/ci.md`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1 and 3.11.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14* (T74; R69): `app` reaches the server of `just dev-app` at `http://api:8080`, by the alias `api` of the devcontainer, and the line of the run names it; В65, which named the address `hostname -i`, left table 3 with В64, recorded by `spec` in Д227 spec, and В67 carries the alias there.

## The `spec` tag: v0.16.7, and `tutor` by its kinds of call

**R68. `api` moves to `spec` v0.16.7, the latest tag of the line 0.16 as of 2026-11-13, and to the newest releases `tutor` v0.1.33 and `learner-model` v0.1.9; `module-kit` v0.3.2 and `engine` v0.1.8 are already the newest. The admission gate asks `tutor` by the kind of call: "explain differently" and the words of a task a tap checks by `ChooseCall(CallHint)`, the words with the work by `ChooseCall(CallWords)`, which holds R84 spec itself, and the quota reserves by the same kind, `CallHint` by a value of its own, $0.0182. The line `ai_usage` carries each model's own time, its retries and when its first text came.** Task T73 of epic E10, 2026-11-13; the move as the first task of the epic was decided by the author when the epic was set up.
- **What the tag brings `api`** (`git diff v0.16.5 v0.16.7` in `spec/`, read 2026-11-13; `gh release verify v0.16.7 -R altnovatrail/spec` — "Release v0.16.7 verified!"):
  - T147 spec — section 10 of the format text: `LICENSE` and `NOTICE` of a module are in the language of the license, not in its explanation language, and the format has no field for it until О-10. `api` serves both as files of the device part (R42) and names no language of them, so nothing changes;
  - T141 spec — WebKit and Firefox in the stage of the kind `typescript`; `api` is of the kind `go`;
  - `contracts/`, `legal/`, `export/`, `admission/`, `types/`, the container and the kind `go` are the same as on v0.16.5: the tag `contracts/v0.16.7` is the commit of `v0.16.7`, and `.claude/settings.json` and `.devcontainer/` need nothing.
- **The libraries** (`git diff v0.1.28 v0.1.33` of `tutor` and `git diff v0.1.7 v0.1.9` of `learner-model`, read 2026-11-13):
  - `tutor` v0.1.29, R105 `tutor`: `claude.New` builds its client with `option.WithoutEnvironmentDefaults()`, `option.WithoutOpenTelemetry()` and the adapters' own HTTP client, `provider.HTTPClient`, whose transport is not `http.DefaultTransport` and waits at most 10 minutes for the headers of an answer. What it takes of the environment is the standard library's — `HTTPS_PROXY`, `NO_PROXY`, `SSL_CERT_FILE`, `SSL_CERT_DIR` —, as every client of the process does. `api` gives `claude.Dial` as it is, and sets no option of the SDK, no variable of a provider and no tracer: `grep` of the code, the scripts and the container finds none. So Д34 is closed by the move;
  - v0.1.30, R106 `tutor`: the kind of call `CallHint` — the hint without the work, on the prompt of the words, one model, no picture — with no expected tokens: `Price(CallHint)` gives the worst alone, $0.512 for Opus 5.5 · `high` (computed 2026-11-13);
  - v0.1.31, R107 `tutor`: `Usage.Latency`, `Usage.Retries` and `Usage.FirstToken`;
  - v0.1.33, R111 `tutor`: one choice per kind of call, which `ChooseCall`, `Price`, `CheckKey` and the call share; for `CallWords` it holds R84 spec — admitted only when `CallHint` chooses the analysis's first model — and otherwise answers the hint's refusal or `model_not_admitted`. Its `go.mod` has `spec` and `spec/contracts` v0.16.7, `module-kit` v0.3.2, and `go.opentelemetry.io/otel` as a direct requirement, a module the binary of `api` held already;
  - `learner-model` v0.1.8, R04 `learner-model`: a module's level forgets after a pause of 14 days since the latest practice of its skills — the weight of its answers falls back to 20 —, so the fold of the evidence changes. The mark of the cache names the version of `learner-model` the binary is built with (R49), so each cache is computed anew at its first read. v0.1.9 changes no Go code, and the `.proto` files `just learner-model-proto` lays are the same.
- **What was decided:**
  - **the gate** — `Gate.Words` asks `ChooseCall(admission.WordsCall(withWork))` — `CallWords` with the work, `CallHint` without it — and admits when the entry has a model. Its own comparison of `hint.Models[0]` with the analysis's model is gone: `tutor` holds it, by the choice the call makes. Today both choose Opus 5.5 · `high` on the prompt of the words, so the gate answers as on v0.1.28;
  - **the quota** — the words of a rung and "explain differently" reserve by the kind of the request they send to `tutor`, as `tutor` picks it: `CallWords` when the crop goes — the words of a handwriting sheet —, `CallHint` without it — a task a tap checks and "explain differently". The kind comes of the request, not of the attempt read before the transaction, so the reserve is that of the call that goes. `tutor` gives `CallHint` no expected price, so its reserve is the value of the kind in `internal/quota`, $0.0182, as the words': the same prompt and model without the picture costs no more, and the words are expected at $0.018164 (computed 2026-11-13). Before, both reserved that expected price of the words, so the reserve grows by $0.000036, and a call that comes back with its usage settles at what it cost;
  - **the stub** (R66) spends the expected price of the kind its request is — `CallWords` with a crop, `CallHint` without —, so "explain differently" on the stub spends nothing priced, and the quota keeps the reserve as spent (R12), as after a model's usage with no price;
  - **the log** — the line `ai_usage` carries per model `model_ms` (`Usage.Latency`, the SDK's retries counted in), `retries` and `first_token_ms`, beside the call's `duration_ms`. They are durations and a count — no identifier, no work, nothing read and no words —, so R09 spec lets them in; of a pair they say which reader held the call.
- **Checked 2026-11-13:** `ci-lint`, `ci-test`, `ci-vuln` — no vulnerability found —, `ci-licenses`, `ci-modules` and `dev-check`, all green. The tests: the gate admits each kind of words exactly when `ChooseCall` of that kind, by a `tutor` made of the same configuration, chooses an entry — for an adult, a child, at the stage full, with no platform key, and by the own keys of Anthropic and OpenAI; `Price(CallHint)` has the worst alone; the words of a sheet reserve by `CallWords`, and those of a task a tap checks and "explain differently" by `CallHint`, which a mutation of the kind fails; the path on the stub spends three sheets and the words at their expected prices and the reserve of `CallHint`; the line `ai_usage` with the three fields. No call of AI, $0.

— *Why:*
- Д34 and Д35 waited for these releases, and the line 0.17 of `spec`, which T74 takes, does not touch them: `tutor` takes only `admissionv0` and `formatv0` of its types;
- the gate by the kind of call: the gate and the call make one choice, so an entry of `hint` on another prompt can no longer admit an "explain differently" that the call then refuses;
- a value of its own for `CallHint`, rather than the price of the words: that is the price of a call with a picture, and R55 keeps the values of the configuration in its table;
- the times in the log: the time of the whole call does not say which reader of a pair was slow or whether the SDK sent it again, and the eval runner of `tutor` measures latency by the same span (R107 `tutor`).

— *Rejected:*
- reserving `CallHint` by its worst price, $0.512: a profile's cap of $0.50 would close at the first "explain differently";
- reserving it by `Price(CallWords)`, as before: one kind of call priced as another, which `tutor` now tells apart;
- the kind of the reserve by `readsAI`, the read of the attempt's task before the transaction, which the gate asks by: a read that fails gives "no work", and the reserve would be the hint's while the crop goes;
- leaving the log as it is: R09 spec does not keep the times out, and the task asked for them where it does not.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-13 amendments to R46, R54, R55, R58 and R66; Д34 and Д35 closed. — RUN.md, T73; [plan/E10.md](plan/E10.md), T73; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status, sections 3.2, 3.5 and 3.6.

## The `spec` tag: v0.17.1, the legal texts by the day and the export of version 2

**R69. `api` moves to `spec` v0.17.1, the latest tag as of 2026-11-14 and the first of the line 0.17 it takes. The legal texts are asked on the day of the request in UTC: the current version of each and its next one, `next_texts`, with `Cache-Control` of an hour and no longer than until 00:00 UTC of the nearest day of a next version, and a request that names a next version before its day is `CAUSE_TEXT_NOT_CURRENT`. A folder of version 3 is read by the rules of the part with `legal.operator`, whose refusal stops the start; the export is of version 2, with `works` empty; `WorkService` answers `unimplemented` behind its checks until T75. The devcontainer gives the container its name in its network, `--network-alias=api`.** Task T74 of epic E10, 2026-11-14; the tag v0.17.1 rather than v0.17.0, and the rows В62, В64 and В65 leaving table 3 for a new В67, were decided by the author in planning, 2026-11-13, by the executor's recommendation.
- **What the tag brings `api`** (`git diff v0.16.7 v0.17.1` in `spec/`, read 2026-11-13; `gh release verify v0.17.1 -R altnovatrail/spec` — "Release v0.17.1 verified!"; Д230 spec):
  - T145 spec — the 2026-11-13 amendments to R130 and R115 spec: version 3 of the part `legal/`, the types `legalv3`. `in_force_since` is the day from which a version applies, 00:00 UTC. A reader reads the part on a day: the current version of a text is the one in force with the highest number whose day has come, and the next is the one in force with the nearest day still ahead, the highest number of that day ([legal/README.md](../spec/legal/README.md), section 5). `GetLegalTexts` gives both, a next text possibly with no current one, and the lifetime of the answer is the rule above ([contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15). The rule `legal.operator` holds every part, `spec`'s own as well as a copy's folder: `privacy-policy` at the place `app` with a version whose day has come, and in each of its versions, drafts too, a heading of the second level that reads exactly `Who we are` — its translations are free of it. A copy whose folder breaks section 5 does not start (section 8 there). `legal.set-texts` asks a version of a set to name versions whose day is not after its own. The vectors of reading have `today` on each case and `next` on its outcome, and a file `operator.yaml` of their own;
  - T148 spec — version 3 of `privacy-policy`, in force from 2026-11-27, which names what goes to the AI for the words after a correct answer; `GetCorrectAnswerWords` takes the call of `tutor` from that day (Д214 spec), outside this task;
  - T144 spec — the 2026-11-13 amendments to R113, R135 and R09 spec: `WorkService` — `PutWork`, `ListWorks`, `GetWork` and `DeleteWork` —, the causes 43 `CAUSE_STORAGE_OFF` and 44 `CAUSE_WORK_TOO_LARGE`, and version 2 of the part `export/`, the types `exportv2`, where a profile's `artefacts` become `works`, its stored works, an array the schema requires;
  - T115 spec — the 2026-11-13 amendment to R21 spec: the devcontainer of the template names the container in its network, `--network-alias=<repository>`, right after its own network (Д195 spec);
  - T116 and T127 spec — the tools `sample-check` and `report-check` of `spec`, and the DPIA against the assessments of Connecticut, Colorado and Montana (the 2026-11-13 amendments to R129 and R140 spec): nothing of `api`'s code;
  - v0.17.1 — the inbox triage at the end of E10 of `spec`, with the 2026-11-13 amendments to R116, R125 and R146 spec: the scans of the consents lie in a bucket of their own, beside the operator's, which `api` takes from `API_CONSENT_FORMS_BUCKET` as before; В64 and В65 went into Д227 spec, В62 into the links it fixed. `contracts/`, `legal/`, `export/` and `types/` are byte for byte those of v0.17.0, and the tag `contracts/v0.17.1` is the commit of `v0.17.1`, 54c5f83;
  - the devcontainer — the draft `just -f spec/justfile new-repo-draft api go 3.5 v0.17.1` differs from `.devcontainer/` by the alias alone, beside the differences of R30, R39 and R67; `.claude/settings.json` and `gopls/` are the draft's.
- **What was decided:**
  - **the day** — the clock of the service that asks: `legal.Service` has `WithClock`, as `account.Service` has, and both are `time.Now` in `serve`, which sets neither; `Spec()` reads the tag on the day of its first call, and `cmd/api` reads a copy's folder on the day it starts. `Texts.On(at)` gives a view of the day of `at` in UTC — `Current`, `Names`, `NamesOne`, `Refs`, `List`, `ListNext` and `KeptFor` —, so the checks and the answers of one request take the moment of its service. `Texts` keeps every version in force of each text, so a request past midnight is checked against the texts of its own day with no restart. The pure `Versions(passport, today)` gives the current and next versions, and the test of the vectors calls it rather than a copy of the rule;
  - **the lifetime** — `KeptFor`: an hour, or less, until 00:00 UTC of the nearest day of a next text of the place `app`, never below zero, in whole seconds rounded down in `max-age`. With no next version it is an hour, and no other rule is needed on the day before a change: a version becomes current at midnight only as a next version that the answer already gives;
  - **the folder** — `Load(at)` and `LoadDir(dir, at)` read the part on the day `api` starts, by the rules in the order of [legal/README.md](../spec/legal/README.md), section 5: files, versions, set-texts, markdown, `legal.operator`, in-force-unchanged. `legal.operator` reads the heading on `goldmark`'s tree, as `legal.markdown` does: a heading of level 2 whose children are all text and together read `Who we are` — GFM splits the text of a heading at the places where an autolink could start, so its pieces are joined, and their character references and backslash escapes are resolved by `goldmark`'s writer of HTML, as micromark resolves them in `spec`'s reader — `## Who&#32;we are` is accepted there and here, `## Who&nbsp;we are` refused (the review). Its refusal names the file or `texts.privacy-policy`, as the vectors do, and `serve` and `api check` fail on it, as on any other rule;
  - **the repeat** — `RequestChildConsent` and `RestateAge` check the version of `parent-consent` inside the action under the idempotency key, at its moment, not before the key. `rpc.Once` keeps no refusal of the action that is not `Kept`, so a repeat under the same key after 00:00 UTC of the day of a new version gets its first answer, not `CAUSE_TEXT_NOT_CURRENT`, and the check and the record take one moment;
  - **the export** — `exportv2`, the version `2` of `format` and of `export_format_version`, and `works` an empty array, never `null`, until T75 keeps the works;
  - **`WorkService`** — the four methods in the table of rules as `can_learn`, the handler `UnimplementedWorkServiceHandler` until T75, as `GetQuota` waited (R62): a profile that is none is `CAUSE_NO_PROFILE` first;
  - **the alias** — `"--network-alias=api"` after `"--network=novatrail-api"`, with the template's comment; README.md names `http://api:8080`, and the line of the run of `scripts/dev.sh` the host `api` and its port, with no scheme: SonarCloud reads a URL of `http` to a host that is not loopback as clear text (S5332). Д31 is closed.
- **Checked 2026-11-14:** `ci-lint`, `ci-test`, `ci-vuln`, `ci-licenses`, `ci-modules` and `dev-check`, all green; `just fuzz 5m FuzzRoutes` — 1.42 million runs in 5 minutes, no failure. The tests: all the vectors of reading, with `today`, the current and next versions equal exactly; the texts of the tag on 2026-11-26 at 12:00 UTC — `privacy-policy` 2 in `texts`, 3 in `next_texts`, `max-age=3600` through the HTTP handler —, at 23:30 — 1800 —, at 23:59:59.5 — 0 —, and on 2026-11-27 at 00:00 — 3 current, no next, 3600; a version 3 named on the 26th and a version 2 on the 27th are `CAUSE_TEXT_NOT_CURRENT`; a text with only a next version is in `next_texts`, not in `texts`, and no request names it; a copy's folder without `## Who we are`, or with the policy only from tomorrow, is refused `legal.operator`, and the heading `Who we are` is read as `spec`'s reader reads it, character references and escapes too; a repeat of `RequestChildConsent` past the day of a new `parent-consent` gets its first answer; the export of version 2 passes its schemas with `"works":[]`; each method of `WorkService` answers `unimplemented`. No call of AI, $0.

— *Why:*
- the day from the service's clock: the account's handlers have one already, and the tests run in parallel, which a clock of the package would not allow;
- all the versions in force kept in `Texts`: the day changes while `api` runs, and an image read once a day would serve the policy 3 a day late or ask a restart at midnight;
- the check after the key: a repeat is the same request, and `rpc.Once` gives a repeat the first answer only of an action that ran;
- refusing the start on `legal.operator`: [legal/README.md](../spec/legal/README.md), section 8, asks it of every reader, and a copy with no `Who we are` tells its users no operator;
- `works` empty: the schema of version 2 requires the array, and a profile with no stored works has none.

— *Rejected:*
- v0.17.0: v0.17.1 changes documents only and records the rows of `api` that the triage took;
- the same tag with renames only: version 3 of the policy would become current on the day of the image, not on 2026-11-27;
- a clock of the package `legal`: a state of the whole package that parallel tests share;
- serving a copy with no current policy, rather than a failed start, when its folder breaks `legal.operator`: [legal/README.md](../spec/legal/README.md), section 8, does not allow it;
- the texts checked before the key, as before: a repeat past midnight would get a refusal that its first answer did not;
- `works` absent until T75: the schema requires it.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-14 amendments to R33, R39, R52, R55, R59 and R67; Д31 closed; В62, В64 and В65 leave table 3, and a new В67 «В реестр spec» — the alias, for Д227 spec. — RUN.md, T74; [plan/E10.md](plan/E10.md), T74; README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status, sections 3.1, 3.9, 3.10 and 3.11.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14* (T75; R70): `WorkService` is built, and **`WorkService`** and **the export** above no longer hold: its four methods answer behind the same checks, `unimplemented` no more, and `works` holds each stored work of the profile, empty only when it has none. The placeholder test of `internal/account` is gone, its methods tested in `internal/learning`.

## Stored works in code

**R70. `api` keeps the stored works of `WorkService` in its own database at Neon: the table `works`, one work by attempt, the last one put, as the contract's `StoredWork` in binary protobuf built anew of the fields the contract names, beside its kind by the attempt's task, its size and when it was kept. A work put holds the profile's row `FOR SHARE`, and switching the storage off erases the works in its transaction after it updates that row; the deletion of a profile or of the account takes them by the cascade. The export of version 2 writes them newest first. `api` has no limit of its own on the number or the sum of a profile's works.** Task T75 of epic E10, 2026-11-14; the database rather than a bucket, and no limit of its own, were decided by the author in planning, by the executor's recommendation.
- **What the contract asks** (`work.proto` and the [contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15, at v0.17.1; the 2026-11-13 amendment to R113 spec): `PutWork`, a learning request, while the storage is on, refused `CAUSE_STORAGE_OFF` and, over 4,194,304 bytes of the history, the contexts and the picture, `CAUSE_WORK_TOO_LARGE`; a form refused `invalid_argument` with no `Refusal`; the kind and when it was kept set by `api`; `ListWorks` and `GetWork` by the learner or by an adult with `can_manage`; `DeleteWork` by whoever decides the storage, as `SetWorkStorage`; no idempotency key; the history and the contexts in the export as the JSON their bytes hold, the picture in base64. storage-consent 1 promises "our database at Neon", that "Nobody reads the works we keep", that the server "never sends them to an AI provider", and that switching off "erases the copies on our server at once, and the copies for recovery within a day".
- **What was decided:**
  - **where** — the database of `api`. The copies for recovery are Neon's history, whose window is 1 day on the deployment's Launch plan (R05). Switching off erases the works in its own transaction, the deletion of a profile or of the account by `ON DELETE CASCADE` of both references, and `api sweep` needs no step;
  - **the table** — migration `0017`: `works (profile_id, attempt_id, kind, size_bytes, work, stored_at)`, the key `(profile_id, attempt_id)`, the references to `profiles` and to `attempts (profile_id, id)`, `kind` one of `handwriting`, `essay` and `drawing`, `size_bytes` from 0 to 4,194,304, and the index `(profile_id, stored_at DESC, attempt_id)` of the list. A repeat of `PutWork` is an upsert, and `stored_at` is the time of the last one;
  - **the bytes** — a `StoredWork` of `attempt_id`, `history`, each submission's `context` and `history_length`, and `picture`, with no `kind` and no `stored_at`, which come of the columns. Connect's binary codec keeps the fields it does not know — `connectproto.NewBinaryCodec` without `DiscardUnknown` —: the message as it came would keep them, and `GetWork` would give them back past the count of 4 MiB. A `kind` or `stored_at` that `app` sends is not taken, so `app` may put back a work as `GetWork` gave it;
  - **the race with switching off** — `PutWork` runs under `store.WriteUnderAccount` and holds the profile's row `FOR SHARE`, which conflicts with the `UPDATE profiles` of `SwitchWorkStorageOff`; `FOR KEY SHARE` would not, since that update takes `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`. So switching off updates the consent's record, then the profile, then erases the works, and a comment says why: erased first, a work put between the erasure and the update would stay after the switch, or the two would deadlock on its row. The locks go in the order of the deletion: the account, then the profile. The work is encoded before the transaction;
  - **`PutWork`**, in this order: the consent and the age, by `rpc.Learner`; an attempt ID that is not canonical, empty too, `CAUSE_NOT_FOUND`, as `hold()` reads one; the size, `CAUSE_WORK_TOO_LARGE`; the form — the history a JSON array and each context a JSON object, in UTF-8, and the picture with PNG's signature, as the contract names it, and each `history_length` not below zero, which the schema of the export asks, `minimum: 0` —, or `invalid_argument` naming the field; an attempt not the profile's, `CAUSE_NOT_FOUND`; the kind; and in the transaction the storage off, `CAUSE_STORAGE_OFF`;
  - **the kind** — by the task of the attempt's package in `set.Packages`, whatever its status, withdrawn too: an answer slot with a rubric makes an essay, one checked by a key or a predicate a handwriting, and none a drawing. A digest the index does not know is a deployment going on, `unavailable` with `CAUSE_PACKAGE_UNKNOWN`, as at every request on an attempt. A package out of the window, or a task its package does not hold, leaves the kind of the work kept of the attempt before, and with none `PutWork` is `failed_precondition` with no `Refusal`. The contract names neither case: В68;
  - **`ListWorks` and `GetWork`** — `ACTION_LEARNING` by `rpc.Learner`, `ACTION_ADULT` by `can_manage`, `permission_denied` with no `Refusal` without it, as `work.proto` says and `GetQuota` does; any other action `invalid_argument`. The list is without the bytes; `GetWork` of an attempt with no work kept, or not the profile's, is `CAUSE_NOT_FOUND`;
  - **`DeleteWork`** — the right first, as `SetWorkStorage`: the adult's `can_manage`, the learner's request by `rpc.Learner`, since section 15 of the contracts text names `DeleteWork` among none of the requests a waiting consent lets through. Then who decides, `store.WorkDecider`, read with no lock: the consent held `FOR UPDATE` would wait in the other order than the deletion of a profile, which holds the profile and reaches the consent by the cascade. The other kind of action is `CAUSE_LEARNER_DECIDES` or `CAUSE_ADULT_NEEDED`, an attempt not the profile's `CAUSE_NOT_FOUND`, and no work kept is "erased" all the same. A learner who decides, while their consent waits, gets `CAUSE_NO_CONSENT`: the text lets switching off through, not `DeleteWork`, so they erase all the works at once but not one — В68;
  - **the code** — in `internal/learning`, the package that holds the set of packages and `rubric(task)`, with `(*Service).Works(db, rights)`; `serve` wires it as `rpc.Handlers.Work`;
  - **the export** — `store.ExportProfile` reads the works in the export's snapshot, newest first, and `internal/export` unpacks each into `exportv2.Work`: the history and the contexts as `json.RawMessage`, the picture in standard base64, `submissions` never `null`. The form `PutWork` checks is what lets the export write a work a device sent; `FuzzStoredWork` of `internal/export` holds it, through `learning.WorkMalformed` and the export's own unpacking. The export stays one response, with no streaming: a work is in memory five or six times over — the row, the decoded message, the base64, the JSON of the profile, the zip and the response —, and a response of Cloud Run over HTTP/1 is at most 32 MiB (sources 11.8), so a profile of many large works may not export. Option (a) of Т-40 may take the works out of `api.zip` altogether: В68, Д37;
  - **no limit of its own** — one work by attempt and 4 MiB a work bound it. A limit on the sum is option (d) of Т-40, `spec`'s open question: `api`'s numbers go there, В68, and the risk is Д37;
  - **Д12** closes whole: an essay is stored as a stored work, its strokes and its picture, by the choice of whoever consents, and the server holds neither what was read nor the words of the feedback anywhere (R113 spec; R15);
  - **found on the way** — `SetWorkStorage` and `RestateAge` hold the consent `FOR UPDATE` and then change the profile, while the deletion of a profile holds the profile `FOR UPDATE` and reaches the consent by the cascade, so the two may deadlock. Postgres breaks one side, which is `unavailable`, `app` repeats it, and no erasure is lost. It is not fixed here (rule 1 of RUN.md): Д38.
- **Checked 2026-11-14:** `ci-lint`, `ci-test`, `ci-vuln`, `ci-licenses` and `dev-check`, all green, `dev-check` with migration `0017` applied on the dev database; `just fuzz 5m FuzzStoredWork` — 3.89 million runs in 5 minutes, no failure. The tests: a work put, listed, read back by the learner and by the adult, put again in its place, erased, and its erasure repeated "erased"; a `kind`, a `stored_at` and a field the contract does not name, sent, are not kept; the kinds essay and handwriting on the packages of the tests, and drawing on tasks with nothing checked; 4,194,304 bytes kept and 4,194,305 refused — by the picture, by one more context, and before a form wrong too —, the work before staying; each wrong form `invalid_argument`; an attempt ID not canonical, of no attempt or of another profile "not found"; the storage off, a digest the index does not know, a package out of the window with a work before and without, a profile with no consent; an adult without `can_manage`, and the other kind of action at `DeleteWork`, a learner's own consent too; switching off, `DeleteProfile` and `DeleteAccount` leave no work; a work put and the switch off, each not committed, make the other wait for the lock, `55P03` by `lock_timeout`; a profile that is none or another account's is `CAUSE_NO_PROFILE` at each method; each statement that fails makes its own request `unavailable`, and a work whose bytes are no message fails `GetWork` and the export; the export of a profile with two works passes the schema of version 2, newest first, the history and the context as JSON and the picture in base64; no history, context or picture in the log. No call of AI, $0.

— *Why:*
- the database: the text in force names it, and its erasure and deletion are those of every other record of the profile, in one transaction, with the history window the text promises;
- the profile's row as the lock: the switch changes it, so one lock orders a work put and the switch with no table or advisory lock of its own;
- the message built anew: what is kept and counted is what the contract names, and nothing more reaches `GetWork` or the export;
- the form checked at `PutWork`: a work the export cannot write as JSON would break the export of the whole profile, later, for a request that is not the device's;
- the order of the checks: a refusal by the work itself comes before anything is read of the attempt, and the transaction holds only what needs the lock.

— *Rejected:*
- a bucket of Cloud Storage: `infra` has none (Д29), its soft delete keeps an object 7 days by default, against "within a day", the erasure would go outside the transaction, and the text in force would need a new version (R130 spec);
- several works by attempt, or the versions of one: the contract says the last one put stands;
- the history and the contexts in columns of `jsonb`: `jsonb` reorders the keys and drops the duplicates, and the export would no longer give them as the canvas gave them;
- the message of the request kept as it came: the unknown fields above;
- `FOR KEY SHARE`, or the erasure before the profile's update: the race above;
- the consent held in `DeleteWork`: the deadlock above;
- a limit of `api`'s own, or the export streamed now: both are Т-40's, which `spec` decides;
- the kind sent by `app`: the contract sets it by the task.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-14 amendments to R15, R21, R27, R39, R52 and R69; Д12 closed, new Д37 and Д38; В68 «В реестр spec». — RUN.md, T75; [plan/E10.md](plan/E10.md), T75; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status, sections 1, 2, 3.2, 3.8, 3.9, 3.10 and 3.11; [sources.md](sources.md), 11.8.

## The board module

**R71. `modules.json` pins `modules` v0.2.7 — the latest release as of 2026-11-14 and the first with the module of `tutor`'s board, — with the index `sha256:6c46a1a9…2083a`: in window 0.4 it has six packages, and the current versions are those of R53 and `novatrail/check-board` 0.1.0. The test of the job "Modules" walks the owner's path on the board module too, in the dev mode. Wherever the least stage of admission is `full` — on every deployment — `api` refuses its tasks "not admitted", until a model of `tutor`'s admission table passes that stage and platform AI opens to others than the author.** Task T76 between epics, 2026-11-14: row 6.17 of `COORDINATION.md` and the 2026-11-14 amendment to R76 spec, in `spec` v0.17.2, read on GitHub; the submodule stays at v0.17.1.
- **What the release carries** (the indexes of v0.2.5, v0.2.6 and v0.2.7 and the archive of `check-board` in v0.2.7, read 2026-11-14):
  - the index of v0.2.6 is the bytes of v0.2.5, and that of v0.2.7 is it with one more entry: `novatrail/check-board` 0.1.0, format 0.4, in v0.2.7. The other packages and their current versions do not change;
  - `check-board` 0.1.0 — "Задачи на листе", mathematics, for adults, explanations in `ru`: the eleven tasks of `internal/board/tasks.go` of `tutor`, `equation-1` to `album`, each on `handwriting_sheet`, one item and one answer slot bound `anywhere`, its check `number`, with a solution, traps and leak phrases, with no leading question or worked example; one lesson, `board`, of the eleven in the board's order; four skills, no `teaches`. Its device part is `LICENSE` alone, the same file as the other five packages';
  - `module-kit` v0.3.2 reads it with no change: `api packages` lays six packages and 1179 files, 17 MB, as many files as v0.2.5, since the one new file is already there under its digest.
- **What was decided:**
  - **the pinning** — the rules of R07 and R41 are the same;
  - **the release test** — `TestThePinnedReleaseIsLearnedOn` runs the subtest "the board module", `boardIsLearnedOn`, on the gate and the stub of `tutor` as `just dev` builds them (R66): every task and the lesson of the module reach the engine for an adult on platform AI, `ListCatalog` gives its current version, `ConnectModule`, `OpenLesson` of `board` and its first task segment begins; a tall crop is "not yet" with one wrong answer, `GetRungWords` on the work gives the stub's words, a wide crop closes the attempt "correct", and `GetProgress` writes its evidence;
  - **where `api` answers "not admitted"** — the module's tasks are all read by AI, so:
    - at the stage `full` `BeginAttempt` is `failed_precondition` with `CAUSE_NOT_ADMITTED`, by platform AI and by Anthropic's own key alike; the engine gets none of its tasks or lessons, and with it alone connected `ChooseNext` gives "nothing" with "not admitted". `author` is refused where `K_SERVICE` or `CLOUD_RUN_JOB` is set (R45), and `tutor` v0.1.33 admits no model at `full` (the 2026-11-08 amendment to R76 spec). The deployment has no facts and no key of a provider either, each of which closes platform AI by itself (R46; the fourth 2026-11-12 amendment to R76 spec). So on the site `infra` rolls out none of its tasks begins until platform AI opens to others than the author;
    - `GetCorrectAnswerWords` is the outcome "not admitted", as on every module (Д26);
    - a child does not see the module at all: its audience is adults, so it is not in the child's catalog, and that is no refusal of admission;
    - locally — `just dev`, `just dev-ai` and `just dev-app`, at the stage `author` with the facts for an adult — every task begins;
  - **README** — after `modules.json` changes, `just packages` lays the packages into an empty folder only, so the old folder is removed first; `just dev-app` and `just dev-check` lay their own.
- **Checked on 2026-11-14:** `release-verify modules v0.2.7` — "signed by the shared release.yml of .github v0.1.15"; `modules-check` against `main`'s v0.2.5 prints the four current versions; `just ci-modules` — the release test and its subtest pass; at the stage `full` a test outside the suite, deleted after, got `CAUSE_NOT_ADMITTED` for both routes and "nothing" from `ChooseNext`; `just ci-test`, `just ci-lint` and `just dev-check`. No call of AI, $0.

— *Why:*
- v0.2.7 is the newest release and the first with the module, on the same format;
- the subtest on the stub: the stub reads by the key, with no provider, through `tutor`'s own admission and prices, the way the author's `just dev` runs; the board's tasks have no tap to check them by;
- a subtest of the same test: `ci-modules` runs that one test by name and looks for the line of its pass, which a failed subtest withholds.

— *Rejected:*
- v0.2.6 — it has no package of its own;
- a reader of the test, as `WithReader`, in place of the stub: it would hold neither `tutor`'s admission nor the dev mode the author runs;
- a test that `full` refuses the module: it would fail on the good news that a model passed the stage, and the test of the gate on the vectors, `TestATaskOfHandwritingAsksTheGate`, holds the rule;
- moving the submodule to v0.17.2: it changes only `COORDINATION.md` and `spec`'s log, and a move to a tag is a task of its own.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-14 amendments to R07, R41 and R53; В69 «В реестр spec». — `modules.json`; `internal/learning/release_test.go`, `stub_test.go`, `learning_test.go`; RUN.md, T76; [plan/E11.md](plan/E11.md), T76; README.md; CLAUDE.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1 and 3.3.
